Implement the following plan:

# 差分比較機能 サイドバイサイド再構築

## Context

現在の差分比較は「統合ビュー（1つのキャンバスにベースと比較先を重ねて表示）」だが、追加/削除ノードが重なると見づらい問題がある。ユーザーがサイドバイサイド方式のモックアップを作成し、全面的に再構築することを決定。

## 新デザイン概要

```
+-------------------------------------------------------------+
|  ツールバー（フィルタ: ☑追加 ☑削除 ☑変更）                    |
+-----------+--+----------------------------------------------+
| ← 戻る    |  |                                              |
| [base.fmd]|  | [compare.fmd]                                |
|           |  |                                               |
| ベース     |  |  比較先キャンバス                              |
| キャンバス  |  |  ADDEDバッジ(緑グロー)                        |
| DELETEDバッジ| |  MODIFIEDバッジ(黄グロー)                     |
| (赤グロー)  |  |                                              |
|           |  |                                               |
+-----------+--+----------------------------------------------+
| Source Code Diff                          [YAML|Mermaid] [×] |
|  1   direction: TD          |  1   direction: TD            |
|  2   nodes:                 |  2   nodes:                   |
|  3 -   label: A             |  3 +   label: テキスト編集     |
+-----------+--+----------------------------------------------+
```

## 実装ステップ

### Step 1: 共有ユーティリティ抽出

**新規: `src/lib/diff/buildDiffNodes.ts`**
- `buildNodeFromLayout(id, n, extraStyle?)` — DiffCanvas.tsxから移動
- `buildPlainNodesEdges(layout)` — DiffCanvas.tsxから移動
- `DIFF_COLORS` 定数 — DiffCanvas.tsxから移動

**変更: `src/lib/diff/formatTextDiff.ts`**
- `computeLineDiff()` をexport化（Mermaid diffでも再利用）

### Step 2: DiffBadgeOverlay作成

**新規: `src/components/diffComparison/DiffBadgeOverlay.tsx`**
- `useViewport()` でフロー座標→スクリーン座標変換
- 各変更ノードの右上に小バッジ（ADDED/DELETED/MODIFIED）
- グロー効果: `box-shadow: 0 0 8px <color>`
- side="base"時: DELETEDとMODIFIEDバッジのみ
- side="compare"時: ADDEDとMODIFIEDバッジのみ

### Step 3: DiffCanvas全面書き直し

**書き直し: `src/components/diffComparison/DiffCanvas.tsx`**

構成:
```
DiffCanvas (ReactFlowProvider不要 — 内部で各自持つ)
  ├── 左: DiffSingleCanvas (ReactFlowProvider内包, side="base")
  │   ├── ReactFlow (読み取り専用)
  │   ├── Panel: ファイル名ラベル
  │   ├── Panel: 戻るボタン (左のみ)
  │   └── DiffBadgeOverlay
  └── 右: DiffSingleCanvas (ReactFlowProvider内包, side="compare")
      ├── ReactFlow (読み取り専用)
      ├── Panel: ファイル名ラベル
      └── DiffBadgeOverlay
```

ビューポート同期:
- 外側で `sharedViewport` state管理
- `onViewportChange` で変更検知 → 他方に `setViewport()` で反映
- `isSyncingRef` (useRef) でループ防止
- 初期表示: 両レイアウトを包含するバウンディングボックスで `fitView`

ノード表示:
- base側: baseLayout全ノード表示。deleted=通常表示、added=非表示
- compare側: compareLayout全ノード表示。added=通常表示、deleted=非表示
- modified: 各側でそれぞれの版を表示

Props:
```typescript
interface DiffCanvasProps {
  baseLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
  compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
  diffResult: DiffResult;
  filters: DiffFilters;
  onExit: () => void;
  baseFileName: string;
  compareFileName: string;
}
```

### Step 4: DiffTextPanelリファクタ

**変更: `src/components/diffComparison/DiffTextPanel.tsx`**

- 上部にYAML/Mermaid切替タブ追加
- YAML: 現在のunified diff（そのまま維持）
- Mermaid: サイドバイサイドdiff
  - `buildPlainNodesEdges(layout)` → `generateMermaid()` で各側のMermaid生成
  - `computeLineDiff()` で行差分計算
  - 左右カラムで表示
- 折りたたみは親（EditorLayout）のResizablePanelで制御

### Step 5: EditorLayout レイアウト変更

**変更: `src/components/layout/EditorLayout.tsx`**

diff compareステップのレイアウト:
```
現在: ResizablePanelGroup(horizontal) → DiffCanvas | DiffTextPanel
新:   ResizablePanelGroup(vertical)   → DiffCanvas / DiffTextPanel
```

- orientation を `"horizontal"` → `"vertical"` に変更
- 上: 65%, 下: 35% のデフォルト分割
- 下部パネルに折りたたみ/展開トグル
- `diffFlashTarget`, `diffFlashSeqRef` 関連のstateを削除（バッジ方式では不要）

### Step 6: i18n翻訳キー更新

**変更: `src/lib/i18n/locales.ts`**

追加:
- `diffSourceCode`: "Source Code Diff" / "ソースコード差分"

削除:

### Step 7: エクスポート更新・クリーンアップ

- `src/components/diffComparison/index.ts` 更新

## 詳細ファイル構造

### 1. src/lib/diff/types.ts（55行）
**役割**: 差分比較の型定義

```typescript
- DiffKind: "added" | "deleted" | "modified" | "unchanged"
- NodeDiff: ノード単位の差分情報（baseNode/compareNode + changedFields）
- EdgeDiff: エッジ単位の差分情報（edgeKey で識別）
- ComponentDefDiff: コンポーネント定義の差分
- DiffResult: nodeDiffs + edgeDiffs + componentDefDiffs の統合結果
- DiffFilters: { added, deleted, modified } チェックボックス状態
- DEFAULT_DIFF_FILTERS: すべてデフォルトで true
- FlowmaidFileData: パース済み .flowmaid ファイルの構造
```

### 2. src/lib/diff/computeDiff.ts（137行）
**役割**: 差分検出のコア ロジック（Pure Function）

```typescript
- deepEqual(a, b): 再帰的オブジェクト比較
- computeNodeDiffs(base, compare): 
  - ノードID をマッチングキーに使用
  - added / deleted / modified / unchanged を判定
  - label 変更のみを差分として検出
- computeEdgeDiffs(base, compare):
  - エッジキー（source-target-handle組み合わせ）でマッチング
  - added / deleted 検出のみ（modification なし）
- computeComponentDefDiffs(baseDefs, compareDefs):
  - 定義ID をマッチングキー
  - name, entry/exit, nodes, edges, direction の変更を追跡
- computeDiff(base, compare): 統合エントリポイント
```

### 3. src/lib/diff/formatTextDiff.ts（171行）
**役割**: DiffResult を git diff 風テキストに変換

```typescript
- DiffTextLine: { type, text, lineNumber, targetId, targetType, diffKind }
- computeLineDiff(baseLines, compareLines):
  - LCS（最長共通部分列）アルゴリズムで unified diff を生成
  - 出力: { type: "added"|"deleted"|"context", text, baseLine?, compareLine? }[]
- layoutToYaml(layout): YAML 文字列に変換
- findTargetForLine(text, contextStack):
  - YAML 構造からノード/エッジ ID を抽出してクリック対象を特定
  - contextStack でインデント深度をトラッキング
- updateContextStack(line, stack):
  - YAML の「nodes:」「edges:」などトップレベルキーで context リセット
  - 2段階目のキー（ノードID）まで追跡
- formatTextDiff(result, filters, baseLayout, compareLayout):
  - フィルタ適用しながらテキスト行を生成
  - lineNumber, targetId, targetType を各行に付与
```

### 4. src/components/diffComparison/DiffImportPanel.tsx（272行）
**役割**: Step 1 - ファイル選択 UI

```typescript
- FileMeta: fileSize, lastModified, nodeCount, edgeCount
- FileSlot コンポーネント:
  * ドラッグ&ドロップ対応（isDragOver 状態管理）
  * ファイル選択ボタン + 入力フィールド
  * handleFile(file): FileReader で .flowmaid を読込、parseLayoutOnly() で parse
  * 「現在の編集内容を使用」: zustand store から serialize()→parseLayoutOnly()
  * showUseCurrentCanvas={true} は左側のみ
- DiffImportPanel（外側）:
  * 左右 FileSlot × 2 の並列レイアウト
  * 下部の [比較実行] ボタン（disabled 制御）
  * 左上の [← 戻る] ボタン
  * Props: baseLayout/compareLayout + onBaseSelect/onCompareSelect + onRunCompare/onExit
```

### 5. src/components/diffComparison/DiffCanvas.tsx（624行）
**役割**: Step 2 左ペイン - キャンバス差分表示（読み取り専用）

```typescript
- DiffViewMode: "diff" | "base" | "compare" (ToggleGroup で切替)
- GROUPING_THRESHOLD: 100px - 近接差分をグルーピング
- FLASH_COUNT: 3回、FLASH_INTERVAL_MS: 200ms
- OVERLAY_PADDING: 16px - オーバーレイ囲み周辺余白
- DIFF_COLORS: { added: 緑, deleted: 赤, modified: 黄, unchanged: transparent }
- DiffGroup: 
  * グルーピング済み差分ユニット（x, y, width, height + label）
  * toggleable=true (modified のみ)
- buildNodeFromLayout(id, n, extraStyle): 
  * FlowmaidNodeLayout → FlowNode に変換
  * style に opacity: 0.4 (削除時) などを追加
- buildDiffGroups(diffResult, filters, baseLayout, compareLayout, t):
  * フィルタ適用した差分をグルーピング
  * 同一 kind で 100px 以内のノードをまとめて単一 group に
  * group.nodeIds で複数ノードを参照
- DiffCanvasInner (memo):
  * ReactFlow の読み取り専用表示（nodesDraggable=false, nodesConnectable=false）
  * viewMode 切替: diff/base/compare
  * groupStates: per-group "base"/"compare" toggle 状態
  * styledNodes: flashingIds に基づきボックスシャドウ追加
  * DiffOverlay: SVG 点線囲み + HTML toggle ボタン群を重ねる
- DiffOverlay (SVG + HTML hybrid):
  * SVG: <g transform="translate(x,y) scale(zoom)"> で座標変換した点線矩形
  * HTML: modified only の 3段UI（< filename >）を screen-space に配置
  * useViewport() で zoom/pan 適用
- DiffCanvas (outer wrapper):
  * ReactFlowProvider でラップ（独立した provider）
```

### 6. src/components/diffComparison/DiffTextPanel.tsx（99行）
**役割**: Step 2 右ペイン - git diff 風テキスト差分表示

```typescript
- LINE_NUM_WIDTH: 4ch
- DiffLine コンポーネント:
  * type: "added"(緑) / "deleted"(赤) / "section" / "context"
  * prefix: "+ " / "- " / "  "
  * lineNumber をパディング表示
  * clickable な行は targetId + targetType 持ち（onItemClick で発火）
- DiffTextPanel:
  * useMemo で formatTextDiff() 実行（diffResult, filters, layouts に依存）
  * lines が空なら「差分はありません」表示
  * otherwise overflow-auto で行リスト表示
  * 各行クリック → onItemClick(targetId, targetType)
```

### 7. src/components/diffComparison/DiffFilterBar.tsx（40行）
**役割**: ツールバー内に埋め込むフィルタチェックボックス群

```typescript
- DiffFilterBar:
  * items: { key: "added"/"deleted"/"modified", label: 翻訳キー }
  * 3つの checkbox（icon + label）を flex gap-3 で横並び
  * onChange → toggle(key) → onFiltersChange({ ...filters, [key]: !filters[key] })
```

### 8. src/components/diffComparison/index.ts（5行）
**役割**: Barrel export

```typescript
export { DiffImportPanel }
export { DiffCanvas }
export { DiffTextPanel }
export { DiffFilterBar }
```

## EditorLayout.tsx との統合（142行修正）

### 差分比較モード state（12個）
```typescript
const [isDiffMode, setIsDiffMode] = useState(false);
const [diffStep, setDiffStep] = useState<"import" | "compare">("import");
const [diffBaseLayout, setDiffBaseLayout] = useState<FlowmaidLayout | null>(null);
const [diffBaseFileName, setDiffBaseFileName] = useState<string | null>(null);
const [diffBaseMeta, setDiffBaseMeta] = useState<FileMeta | null>(null);
const [diffCompareLayout, setDiffCompareLayout] = useState<FlowmaidLayout | null>(null);
const [diffCompareFileName, setDiffCompareFileName] = useState<string | null>(null);
const [diffCompareMeta, setDiffCompareMeta] = useState<FileMeta | null>(null);
const [diffFilters, setDiffFilters] = useState<DiffFilters>(DEFAULT_DIFF_FILTERS);
const [diffResult, setDiffResult] = useState<DiffResult | null>(null);
const [diffFlashTarget, setDiffFlashTarget] = useState<{ id: string; seq: number } | null>(null);
const diffFlashSeqRef = useRef(0);
```

### ページ遷移フロー
```
isDiffMode=false → [差分比較ボタン] → isDiffMode=true, diffStep="import"
  → [比較実行] → diffStep="compare"
  → [← 戻る] → diffStep="import"
  → [← 戻る] → isDiffMode=false
```

### ハンドラ関数（5個）
- `handleEnterDiffMode`: すべてリセットして差分モード開始
- `handleExitDiffMode`: diffStep 判定で戻る / 終了を分岐
- `handleRunDiff`: computeDiff() 実行して diffResult セット
- `handleDiffTextItemClick`: diffFlashSeqRef インクリメント → flashTarget トリガー
- `handleDiff{Base,Compare}Select/Clear`: 各ファイル state 更新

### レイアウト切替（lines 343-386）
```tsx
{isDiffMode ? (
  <div className="flex flex-1 overflow-hidden">
    {diffStep === "import" ? (
      <DiffImportPanel ... />
    ) : diffResult && diffBaseLayout && diffCompareLayout ? (
      <ResizablePanelGroup orientation="horizontal">
        <ResizablePanel defaultSize={60} minSize={30}>
          <DiffCanvas ... />
        </ResizablePanel>
        <ResizableHandle bare />
        <ResizablePanel defaultSize={40} minSize={25}>
          <DiffTextPanel ... />
        </ResizablePanel>
      </ResizablePanelGroup>
    ) : null}
  </div>
) : isBulkEditMode ? (
  // BulkEdit layout...
) : (
  // Normal canvas layout...
)}
```

## 仕様書（specs/diff-comparison.md）

417行の詳細設計書。主な内容:

- **実装方針**: EditorLayout のローカル state で管理（zustand store は汚染しない）
- **画面遷移**: 通常 → Step 1 インポート → Step 2 比較
- **差分検出**:
  - ノード: nodeId でマッチング → added/deleted/modified/unchanged
  - エッジ: (source, target, sourceHandle, targetHandle) でマッチング
  - コンポーネント定義: defId でマッチング
- **ハイライト表示**:
  - 追加: 緑点線（`#22c55e`）
  - 削除: 赤点線（`#ef4444`）
  - 修正: 黄点線（`#eab308`）
  - グルーピング: 100px 以内の同一 kind をまとめる
- **グループトグル**: Modified なノードグループのみ < > で base/compare 切替可能
- **フィルタ**: 3種（added/deleted/modified）をツールバーで on/off 制御
- **クリック体験**: テキスト差分項目クリック → キャンバスの対応ノード点滅

---

## 関連型定義とインポート

```typescript
// src/types/flow
ComponentDefinition: { id, name, version, entryNodeId, exitNodeId, nodes[], edges[], direction? }

// src/lib/flowmaid/schema
FlowmaidLayout: { nodes: {}, edges: {}, componentDefinitions? }
FlowmaidNodeLayout: { position, size?, shape, label, ... colors/styles }
FlowmaidEdgeLayout: { source, target, sourceHandle?, targetHandle?, label?, edgeType, ... }

// src/lib/flowmaid/deserialize
parseLayoutOnly(content: string): FlowmaidLayout
```

---

## パフォーマンス特性

1. **computeDiff**: ファイルロード時に1回だけ実行（pure function、キャッシュ）
2. **formatTextDiff**: useMemo で diffResult 変更時のみ再実行
3. **DiffCanvas**: 
   - useMemo で displayNodes/displayEdges 再計算（baseLayout/compareLayout/diffResult/filters に依存）
   - useCallback で toggleGroup 安定化
   - React.memo で DiffCanvasInner をラップ
4. **DiffOverlay**: SVG は <g transform> で変換（ズーム対応）、HTML toggle UI は useViewport() で座標変換

---

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useState, useCallback } from "react";
     4→import {
     5→  ReactFlow,
     6→  ReactFlowProvider,
     7→  Panel,
     8→  useViewport,
     9→  type Node,
    10→} from "@xyflow/react";
    11→import { ArrowLeft, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";
    12→import { useLocale } from "@/lib/i18n/useLocale";
    13→import type { TranslationKey } from "@/lib/i18n/locales";
    14→import { nodeTypes } from "@/components/nodes/nodeTypes";
    15→import { edgeTypes } from "@/components/edges/edgeTypes";
    16→import type { FlowmaidLayout, FlowmaidNodeLayout } from "@/lib/flowmaid/schema";
    17→import type { DiffResult, DiffFilters, DiffKind } from "@/lib/diff/types";
    18→import { ToggleGroup, ToggleGroupItem } from "@/components/ui/toggle-group";
    19→import type { FlowNode, FlowEdge } from "@/store/types";
    20→
    21→type DiffViewMode = "diff" | "base" | "compare";
    22→
    23→/** Distance threshold for grouping nearby changed nodes (px) */
    24→const GROUPING_THRESHOLD = 100;
    25→
    26→/** Number of times to flash a node when clicked in text panel */
    27→const FLASH_COUNT = 3;
    28→const FLASH_INTERVAL_MS = 200;
    29→
    30→/** Padding around diff overlay rectangles */
    31→const OVERLAY_PADDING = 16;
    32→
    33→const DIFF_COLORS: Record<DiffKind, string> = {
    34→  added: "#22c55e",
    35→  deleted: "#ef4444",
    36→  modified: "#eab308",
    37→  unchanged: "transparent",
    38→};
    39→
    40→// ─── Diff Group (overlay rect + toggle state) ───
    41→
    42→interface DiffGroup {
    43→  key: string;
    44→  kind: DiffKind;
    45→  label: string;
    46→  nodeIds: string[];
    47→  /** Whether this group supports < > toggling */
    48→  toggleable: boolean;
    49→  x: number;
    50→  y: number;
    51→  width: number;
    52→  height: number;
    53→}
    54→
    55→function buildNodeFromLayout(id: string, n: FlowmaidNodeLayout, extraStyle?: React.CSSProperties): FlowNode {
    56→  return {
    57→    id,
    58→    type: n.componentDefinitionId ? "componentInstance" : n.shape,
    59→    position: n.position,
    60→    data: {
    61→      label: n.label,
    62→      shape: n.shape as FlowNode["data"]["shape"],
    63→      fillColor: n.fillColor,
    64→      borderColor: n.borderColor,
    65→      borderWidth: n.borderWidth,
    66→      borderStyle: n.borderStyle,
    67→      fontSize: n.fontSize,
    68→      textColor: n.textColor,
    69→      textAlign: n.textAlign,
    70→      bold: n.bold,
    71→      italic: n.italic,
    72→      underline: n.underline,
    73→    },
    74→    style: {
    75→      ...(n.size ? { width: n.size.width, height: n.size.height } : {}),
    76→      ...extraStyle,
    77→    },
    78→  } as FlowNode;
    79→}
    80→
    81→function buildDiffGroups(
    82→  diffResult: DiffResult,
    83→  filters: DiffFilters,
    84→  baseLayout: FlowmaidLayout,
    85→  compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout,
    86→  t: (key: TranslationKey) => string,
    87→): DiffGroup[] {
    88→  const groups: DiffGroup[] = [];
    89→
    90→  // Collect items with positions for grouping
    91→  const items: { id: string; kind: DiffKind; x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }[] = [];
    92→
    93→  for (const nd of diffResult.nodeDiffs) {
    94→    if (nd.kind === "unchanged") continue;
    95→    // Apply filters by kind
    96→    if (nd.kind === "added" && !filters.added) continue;
    97→    if (nd.kind === "deleted" && !filters.deleted) continue;
    98→    if (nd.kind === "modified" && !filters.modified) continue;
    99→
   100→    // Use base position if available, otherwise compare position
   101→    const nodeLayout = nd.baseNode ?? nd.compareNode;
   102→    if (!nodeLayout) continue;
   103→
   104→    const w = nodeLayout.size?.width ?? 150;
   105→    const h = nodeLayout.size?.height ?? 50;
   106→
   107→    items.push({ id: nd.nodeId, kind: nd.kind, x: nodeLayout.position.x, y: nodeLayout.position.y, w, h });
   108→  }
   109→
   110→  // Group nearby items of the same kind
   111→  const used = new Set<number>();
   112→
   113→  for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
   114→    if (used.has(i)) continue;
   115→
   116→    const group = [items[i]];
   117→    used.add(i);
   118→
   119→    for (let j = i + 1; j < items.length; j++) {
   120→      if (used.has(j)) continue;
   121→      if (items[j].kind !== items[i].kind) continue;
   122→
   123→      const isClose = group.some((g) => {
   124→        const dx = Math.abs((items[j].x + items[j].w / 2) - (g.x + g.w / 2));
   125→        const dy = Math.abs((items[j].y + items[j].h / 2) - (g.y + g.h / 2));
   126→        return dx < GROUPING_THRESHOLD + g.w / 2 + items[j].w / 2 &&
   127→               dy < GROUPING_THRESHOLD + g.h / 2 + items[j].h / 2;
   128→      });
   129→
   130→      if (isClose) {
   131→        group.push(items[j]);
   132→        used.add(j);
   133→      }
   134→    }
   135→
   136→    let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
   137→    const nodeIds: string[] = [];
   138→    for (const item of group) {
   139→      minX = Math.min(minX, item.x);
   140→      minY = Math.min(minY, item.y);
   141→      maxX = Math.max(maxX, item.x + item.w);
   142→      maxY = Math.max(maxY, item.y + item.h);
   143→      nodeIds.push(item.id);
   144→    }
   145→
   146→    const kindLabels: Record<DiffKind, string> = {
   147→      added: t("diffKindAdded"),
   148→      deleted: t("diffKindDeleted"),
   149→      modified: t("diffKindModified"),
   150→      unchanged: "",
   151→    };
   152→
   153→    const kind = group[0].kind;
   154→
   155→    groups.push({
   156→      key: nodeIds.sort().join(","),
   157→      kind,
   158→      label: kindLabels[kind],
   159→      nodeIds,
   160→      toggleable: kind === "modified",
   161→      x: minX - OVERLAY_PADDING,
   162→      y: minY - OVERLAY_PADDING,
   163→      width: maxX - minX + OVERLAY_PADDING * 2,
   164→      height: maxY - minY + OVERLAY_PADDING * 2,
   165→    });
   166→  }
   167→
   168→  return groups;
   169→}
   170→
   171→// ─── Plain layout builder (for base/compare single view) ───
   172→
   173→function buildPlainNodesEdges(layout: FlowmaidLayout): { displayNodes: FlowNode[]; displayEdges: FlowEdge[] } {
   174→  const nodes: FlowNode[] = [];
   175→  const edges: FlowEdge[] = [];
   176→
   177→  for (const [id, n] of Object.entries(layout.nodes)) {
   178→    nodes.push(buildNodeFromLayout(id, n));
   179→  }
   180→
   181→  for (const [id, e] of Object.entries(layout.edges)) {
   182→    edges.push({
   183→      id,
   184→      source: e.source,
   185→      target: e.target,
   186→      type: "labeled",
   187→      sourceHandle: e.sourceHandle,
   188→      targetHandle: e.targetHandle,
   189→      data: {
   190→        label: e.label ?? "",
   191→        edgeType: e.edgeType ?? "bezier",
   192→        markerEnd: e.markerEnd ?? "arrowclosed",
   193→        markerStart: e.markerStart,
   194→        strokeWidth: e.strokeWidth,
   195→        strokeColor: e.strokeColor,
   196→        strokeStyle: e.strokeStyle,
   197→      },
   198→    } as FlowEdge);
   199→  }
   200→
   201→  return { displayNodes: nodes, displayEdges: edges };
   202→}
   203→
   204→// ─── DiffCanvasInner ───
   205→
   206→interface DiffCanvasInnerProps {
   207→  baseLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
   208→  compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
   209→  diffResult: DiffResult;
   210→  filters: DiffFilters;
   211→  flashTarget: { id: string; seq: number } | null;
   212→  onExit: () => void;
   213→  baseFileName: string;
   214→  compareFileName: string;
   215→}
   216→
   217→const DiffCanvasInner = memo(function DiffCanvasInner({
   218→  baseLayout,
   219→  compareLayout,
   220→  diffResult,
   221→  filters,
   222→  flashTarget,
   223→  onExit,
   224→  baseFileName,
   225→  compareFileName,
   226→}: DiffCanvasInnerProps) {
   227→  const { t } = useLocale();
   228→  const [flashingIds, setFlashingIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
   229→  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<DiffViewMode>("diff");
   230→  // Per-group toggle state: key → "base" | "compare"
   231→  const [groupStates, setGroupStates] = useState<Record<string, "base" | "compare">>({});
   232→
   233→  // Build diff groups
   234→  const diffGroups = useMemo(
   235→    () => viewMode === "diff" ? buildDiffGroups(diffResult, filters, baseLayout, compareLayout, t) : [],
   236→    [diffResult, filters, baseLayout, compareLayout, t, viewMode]
   237→  );
   238→
   239→  // Toggle a group's view between base and compare
   240→  const toggleGroup = useCallback((groupKey: string) => {
   241→    setGroupStates((prev) => ({
   242→      ...prev,
   243→      [groupKey]: prev[groupKey] === "compare" ? "base" : "compare",
   244→    }));
   245→  }, []);
   246→
   247→  // Build merged nodes/edges for diff mode
   248→  const { displayNodes, displayEdges } = useMemo(() => {
   249→    if (viewMode === "base") return buildPlainNodesEdges(baseLayout);
   250→    if (viewMode === "compare") return buildPlainNodesEdges(compareLayout);
   251→
   252→    const nodes: FlowNode[] = [];
   253→    const edges: FlowEdge[] = [];
   254→
   255→    // Build a map: nodeId → which group it belongs to
   256→    const nodeToGroup = new Map<string, DiffGroup>();
   257→    for (const group of diffGroups) {
   258→      for (const nid of group.nodeIds) {
   259→        nodeToGroup.set(nid, group);
   260→      }
   261→    }
   262→
   263→    // For each node in the merged set, decide which version to show
   264→    const allNodeIds = new Set([
   265→      ...Object.keys(baseLayout.nodes),
   266→      ...Object.keys(compareLayout.nodes),
   267→    ]);
   268→
   269→    for (const id of allNodeIds) {
   270→      const nd = diffResult.nodeDiffs.find((d) => d.nodeId === id);
   271→      const kind = nd?.kind ?? "unchanged";
   272→      const baseNode = baseLayout.nodes[id];
   273→      const compareNode = compareLayout.nodes[id];
   274→
   275→      if (kind === "unchanged" && baseNode) {
   276→        // Unchanged: show base version
   277→        nodes.push(buildNodeFromLayout(id, baseNode));
   278→        continue;
   279→      }
   280→
   281→      if (kind === "added" && compareNode) {
   282→        if (!filters.added) continue;
   283→        nodes.push(buildNodeFromLayout(id, compareNode));
   284→        continue;
   285→      }
   286→
   287→      if (kind === "deleted" && baseNode) {
   288→        if (!filters.deleted) continue;
   289→        nodes.push(buildNodeFromLayout(id, baseNode, { opacity: 0.4 }));
   290→        continue;
   291→      }
   292→
   293→      if (kind === "modified" && baseNode && compareNode) {
   294→        if (!filters.modified) {
   295→          // Show base version without diff highlight
   296→          nodes.push(buildNodeFromLayout(id, baseNode));
   297→          continue;
   298→        }
   299→        // Toggleable: show base or compare based on group state
   300→        const group = nodeToGroup.get(id);
   301→        const showCompare = group ? groupStates[group.key] === "compare" : false;
   302→        const srcNode = showCompare ? compareNode : baseNode;
   303→        nodes.push(buildNodeFromLayout(id, srcNode));
   304→        continue;
   305→      }
   306→    }
   307→
   308→    // Build edges from base layout
   309→    for (const [id, e] of Object.entries(baseLayout.edges)) {
   310→      const ed = diffResult.edgeDiffs.find((d) => d.edgeKey === id);
   311→      const isDeleted = ed?.kind === "deleted";
   312→
   313→      // Apply filter
   314→      if (isDeleted && !filters.deleted) continue;
   315→
   316→      // Only show if both source/target exist in our node set
   317→      const sourceExists = nodes.some((n) => n.id === e.source);
   318→      const targetExists = nodes.some((n) => n.id === e.target);
   319→      if (!sourceExists || !targetExists) continue;
   320→
   321→      edges.push({
   322→        id,
   323→        source: e.source,
   324→        target: e.target,
   325→        type: "labeled",
   326→        sourceHandle: e.sourceHandle,
   327→        targetHandle: e.targetHandle,
   328→        data: {
   329→          label: e.label ?? "",
   330→          edgeType: e.edgeType ?? "bezier",
   331→          markerEnd: e.markerEnd ?? "arrowclosed",
   332→          markerStart: e.markerStart,
   333→          strokeWidth: e.strokeWidth,
   334→          strokeColor: e.strokeColor,
   335→          strokeStyle: e.strokeStyle,
   336→        },
   337→        style: isDeleted ? { opacity: 0.4 } : undefined,
   338→      } as FlowEdge);
   339→    }
   340→
   341→    // Add compare-only edges (added)
   342→    if (filters.added) {
   343→      const baseEdgeKeys = new Set(Object.keys(baseLayout.edges));
   344→      for (const [id, e] of Object.entries(compareLayout.edges)) {
   345→        if (baseEdgeKeys.has(id)) continue;
   346→        const sourceExists = nodes.some((n) => n.id === e.source);
   347→        const targetExists = nodes.some((n) => n.id === e.target);
   348→        if (!sourceExists || !targetExists) continue;
   349→
   350→        edges.push({
   351→          id,
   352→          source: e.source,
   353→          target: e.target,
   354→          type: "labeled",
   355→          sourceHandle: e.sourceHandle,
   356→          targetHandle: e.targetHandle,
   357→          data: {
   358→            label: e.label ?? "",
   359→            edgeType: e.edgeType ?? "bezier",
   360→            markerEnd: e.markerEnd ?? "arrowclosed",
   361→            markerStart: e.markerStart,
   362→            strokeWidth: e.strokeWidth,
   363→            strokeColor: e.strokeColor,
   364→            strokeStyle: e.strokeStyle,
   365→          },
   366→        } as FlowEdge);
   367→      }
   368→    }
   369→
   370→    return { displayNodes: nodes, displayEdges: edges };
   371→  }, [baseLayout, compareLayout, diffResult, filters, viewMode, diffGroups, groupStates]);
   372→
   373→  // Flash animation
   374→  useEffect(() => {
   375→    if (!flashTarget) return;
   376→    let count = 0;
   377→    const interval = setInterval(() => {
   378→      count++;
   379→      if (count > FLASH_COUNT * 2) {
   380→        clearInterval(interval);
   381→        setFlashingIds(new Set());
   382→        return;
   383→      }
   384→      setFlashingIds(count % 2 === 1 ? new Set([flashTarget.id]) : new Set());
   385→    }, FLASH_INTERVAL_MS);
   386→    return () => clearInterval(interval);
   387→  }, [flashTarget]);
   388→
   389→  const styledNodes = useMemo(() => {
   390→    return displayNodes.map((n) => {
   391→      const isFlashing = flashingIds.has(n.id);
   392→      if (isFlashing) {
   393→        return {
   394→          ...n,
   395→          style: {
   396→            ...n.style,
   397→            boxShadow: "0 0 0 3px var(--color-primary)",
   398→            transition: "box-shadow 0.1s ease",
   399→          },
   400→        };
   401→      }
   402→      return n;
   403→    });
   404→  }, [displayNodes, flashingIds]);
   405→
   406→  return (
   407→    <div className="h-full w-full">
   408→      <ReactFlow
   409→        nodes={styledNodes}
   410→        edges={displayEdges}
   411→        nodeTypes={nodeTypes}
   412→        edgeTypes={edgeTypes}
   413→        nodesDraggable={false}
   414→        nodesConnectable={false}
   415→        elementsSelectable={false}
   416→        connectOnClick={false}
   417→        zoomOnScroll
   418→        panOnDrag
   419→        fitView
   420→        proOptions={{ hideAttribution: true }}
   421→      >
   422→        <Panel position="top-left">
   423→          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   424→            <button
   425→              onClick={onExit}
   426→              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-background/80 border border-border text-xs text-foreground backdrop-blur-sm hover:bg-accent cursor-pointer"
   427→            >
   428→              <ArrowLeft size={14} />
   429→              {t("diffBack")}
   430→            </button>
   431→            <ToggleGroup
   432→              type="single"
   433→              value={viewMode}
   434→              onValueChange={(val) => { if (val) setViewMode(val as DiffViewMode); }}
   435→              className="bg-background/80 border border-border rounded-md backdrop-blur-sm"
   436→            >
   437→              <ToggleGroupItem value="diff" className="h-7 px-2.5 text-xs data-[state=on]:bg-accent">
   438→                {t("diffViewDiff")}
   439→              </ToggleGroupItem>
   440→              <ToggleGroupItem value="base" className="h-7 px-2.5 text-xs data-[state=on]:bg-accent max-w-32 truncate">
   441→                {baseFileName.replace(/\.flowmaid$/, "")}
   442→              </ToggleGroupItem>
   443→              <ToggleGroupItem value="compare" className="h-7 px-2.5 text-xs data-[state=on]:bg-accent max-w-32 truncate">
   444→                {compareFileName.replace(/\.flowmaid$/, "")}
   445→              </ToggleGroupItem>
   446→            </ToggleGroup>
   447→          </div>
   448→        </Panel>
   449→
   450→        {/* Diff overlays with toggle controls */}
   451→        <DiffOverlay
   452→          groups={diffGroups}
   453→          groupStates={groupStates}
   454→          onToggle={toggleGroup}
   455→          baseFileName={baseFileName}
   456→          compareFileName={compareFileName}
   457→        />
   458→      </ReactFlow>
   459→    </div>
   460→  );
   461→});
   462→
   463→// ─── Diff Overlay (SVG outlines + HTML toggle controls) ───
   464→
   465→interface DiffOverlayProps {
   466→  groups: DiffGroup[];
   467→  groupStates: Record<string, "base" | "compare">;
   468→  onToggle: (groupKey: string) => void;
   469→  baseFileName: string;
   470→  compareFileName: string;
   471→}
   472→
   473→function DiffOverlay({ groups, groupStates, onToggle, baseFileName, compareFileName }: DiffOverlayProps) {
   474→  const { x, y, zoom } = useViewport();
   475→
   476→  if (groups.length === 0) return null;
   477→
   478→  const LABEL_FONT_SIZE = 11;
   479→  const LABEL_PADDING_X = 6;
   480→  const LABEL_PADDING_Y = 2;
   481→
   482→  return (
   483→    <>
   484→      {/* SVG dashed outlines */}
   485→      <svg
   486→        style={{
   487→          position: "absolute",
   488→          top: 0,
   489→          left: 0,
   490→          width: "100%",
   491→          height: "100%",
   492→          pointerEvents: "none",
   493→          overflow: "visible",
   494→        }}
   495→      >
   496→        <g transform={`translate(${x},${y}) scale(${zoom})`}>
   497→          {groups.map((group) => {
   498→            const color = DIFF_COLORS[group.kind];
   499→            return (
   500→              <g key={group.key}>
   501→                <rect
   502→                  x={group.x}
   503→                  y={group.y}
   504→                  width={group.width}
   505→                  height={group.height}
   506→                  fill="none"
   507→                  stroke={color}
   508→                  strokeWidth={2 / zoom}
   509→                  strokeDasharray={`${6 / zoom} ${4 / zoom}`}
   510→                  rx={4 / zoom}
   511→                />
   512→                {/* Non-toggleable label (added/deleted) */}
   513→                {!group.toggleable && (
   514→                  <>
   515→                    <rect
   516→                      x={group.x}
   517→                      y={group.y - (LABEL_FONT_SIZE + LABEL_PADDING_Y * 2 + 4) / zoom}
   518→                      width={(group.label.length * LABEL_FONT_SIZE * 0.7 + LABEL_PADDING_X * 2) / zoom}
   519→                      height={(LABEL_FONT_SIZE + LABEL_PADDING_Y * 2) / zoom}
   520→                      fill={color}
   521→                      rx={3 / zoom}
   522→                    />
   523→                    <text
   524→                      x={group.x + LABEL_PADDING_X / zoom}
   525→                      y={group.y - (LABEL_PADDING_Y + 4) / zoom}
   526→                      fill="white"
   527→                      fontSize={LABEL_FONT_SIZE / zoom}
   528→                      fontWeight="bold"
   529→                      fontFamily="system-ui, sans-serif"
   530→                    >
   531→                      {group.label}
   532→                    </text>
   533→                  </>
   534→                )}
   535→              </g>
   536→            );
   537→          })}
   538→        </g>
   539→      </svg>
   540→
   541→      {/* HTML toggle controls for toggleable groups (modified/moved) */}
   542→      {groups.filter((g) => g.toggleable).map((group) => {
   543→        const color = DIFF_COLORS[group.kind];
   544→        const showingCompare = groupStates[group.key] === "compare";
   545→        const currentFileName = showingCompare
   546→          ? compareFileName.replace(/\.flowmaid$/, "")
   547→          : baseFileName.replace(/\.flowmaid$/, "");
   548→
   549→        // Convert flow-space position to screen-space
   550→        const screenX = group.x * zoom + x;
   551→        const screenY = group.y * zoom + y;
   552→
   553→        return (
   554→          <div
   555→            key={group.key}
   556→            style={{
   557→              position: "absolute",
   558→              left: screenX,
   559→              top: screenY - 28,
   560→              zIndex: 5,
   561→              pointerEvents: "auto",
   562→            }}
   563→          >
   564→            <div
   565→              className="flex items-center gap-0 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold select-none"
   566→              style={{ backgroundColor: color }}
   567→            >
   568→              {/* Label */}
   569→              <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 text-white whitespace-nowrap">
   570→                {group.label}
   571→              </span>
   572→              {/* < button */}
   573→              <button
   574→                onClick={() => {
   575→                  if (showingCompare) onToggle(group.key);
   576→                }}
   577→                className="px-0.5 py-0.5 text-white/70 hover:text-white cursor-pointer"
   578→                style={{ opacity: showingCompare ? 1 : 0.4 }}
   579→              >
   580→                <ChevronLeft size={12} />
   581→              </button>
   582→              {/* File name */}
   583→              <span className="px-1 py-0.5 text-white/90 max-w-24 truncate">
   584→                {currentFileName}
   585→              </span>
   586→              {/* > button */}
   587→              <button
   588→                onClick={() => {
   589→                  if (!showingCompare) onToggle(group.key);
   590→                }}
   591→                className="px-0.5 py-0.5 text-white/70 hover:text-white cursor-pointer"
   592→                style={{ opacity: showingCompare ? 0.4 : 1 }}
   593→              >
   594→                <ChevronRight size={12} />
   595→              </button>
   596→            </div>
   597→          </div>
   598→        );
   599→      })}
   600→    </>
   601→  );
   602→}
   603→
   604→// ─── Outer wrapper ───
   605→
   606→interface DiffCanvasProps {
   607→  baseLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
   608→  compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
   609→  diffResult: DiffResult;
   610→  filters: DiffFilters;
   611→  flashTarget: { id: string; seq: number } | null;
   612→  onExit: () => void;
   613→  baseFileName: string;
   614→  compareFileName: string;
   615→}
   616→
   617→export const DiffCanvas = memo(function DiffCanvas(props: DiffCanvasProps) {
   618→  return (
   619→    <ReactFlowProvider>
   620→      <DiffCanvasInner {...props} />
   621→    </ReactFlowProvider>
   622→  );
   623→});
   624→

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import { useMemo } from "react";
     4→import type { DiffResult, DiffFilters } from "@/lib/diff/types";
     5→import type { FlowmaidLayout } from "@/lib/flowmaid/schema";
     6→import { formatTextDiff, type DiffTextLine } from "@/lib/diff/formatTextDiff";
     7→import { useLocale } from "@/lib/i18n/useLocale";
     8→
     9→interface DiffTextPanelProps {
    10→  diffResult: DiffResult;
    11→  filters: DiffFilters;
    12→  baseLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
    13→  compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
    14→  onItemClick: (targetId: string, targetType: "node" | "edge" | "componentDef") => void;
    15→}
    16→
    17→/** Width reserved for line number gutter (ch units) */
    18→const LINE_NUM_WIDTH = 4;
    19→
    20→function DiffLine({ line, onClick }: { line: DiffTextLine; onClick?: () => void }) {
    21→  const isClickable = !!line.targetId && !!onClick;
    22→
    23→  const lineNumStr = line.lineNumber != null
    24→    ? String(line.lineNumber).padStart(LINE_NUM_WIDTH, " ")
    25→    : " ".repeat(LINE_NUM_WIDTH);
    26→
    27→  let prefix = "  ";
    28→  let rowClass = "text-foreground/70";
    29→  let bgClass = "";
    30→
    31→  switch (line.type) {
    32→    case "added":
    33→      prefix = "+ ";
    34→      rowClass = "text-green-600 dark:text-green-400";
    35→      bgClass = "bg-green-500/10";
    36→      break;
    37→    case "deleted":
    38→      prefix = "- ";
    39→      rowClass = "text-red-600 dark:text-red-400";
    40→      bgClass = "bg-red-500/10";
    41→      break;
    42→    case "section":
    43→      rowClass = "text-muted-foreground font-semibold";
    44→      bgClass = "bg-muted/50";
    45→      break;
    46→    case "context":
    47→    default:
    48→      break;
    49→  }
    50→
    51→  return (
    52→    <div
    53→      className={`flex font-mono text-xs leading-5 ${bgClass} ${isClickable ? "cursor-pointer hover:bg-muted/30" : ""}`}
    54→      onClick={isClickable ? onClick : undefined}
    55→    >
    56→      <span className="select-none text-muted-foreground/50 pr-2 text-right shrink-0" style={{ width: `${LINE_NUM_WIDTH + 1}ch` }}>
    57→        {lineNumStr}
    58→      </span>
    59→      <span className={`select-none shrink-0 ${rowClass}`}>{prefix}</span>
    60→      <span className={`${rowClass} whitespace-pre`}>{line.text || "\u00A0"}</span>
    61→    </div>
    62→  );
    63→}
    64→
    65→export function DiffTextPanel({ diffResult, filters, baseLayout, compareLayout, onItemClick }: DiffTextPanelProps) {
    66→  const { t } = useLocale();
    67→
    68→  const lines = useMemo(
    69→    () => formatTextDiff(diffResult, filters, baseLayout, compareLayout),
    70→    [diffResult, filters, baseLayout, compareLayout]
    71→  );
    72→
    73→  if (lines.length === 0) {
    74→    return (
    75→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-full text-muted-foreground text-sm">
    76→        {t("diffNoDifferences")}
    77→      </div>
    78→    );
    79→  }
    80→
    81→  return (
    82→    <div className="h-full overflow-auto bg-background">
    83→      <div className="py-1 px-2">
    84→        {lines.map((line, i) => (
    85→          <DiffLine
    86→            key={i}
    87→            line={line}
    88→            onClick={
    89→              line.targetId && line.targetType
    90→                ? () => onItemClick(line.targetId!, line.targetType!)
    91→                : undefined
    92→            }
    93→          />
    94→        ))}
    95→      </div>
    96→    </div>
    97→  );
    98→}
    99→

     1→import { stringify } from "yaml";
     2→import type { FlowmaidLayout } from "@/lib/flowmaid/schema";
     3→import type { DiffResult, DiffFilters, DiffKind } from "./types";
     4→
     5→export interface DiffTextLine {
     6→  type: "added" | "deleted" | "context" | "section";
     7→  text: string;
     8→  lineNumber?: number;
     9→  /** Node or edge ID for click-to-flash */
    10→  targetId?: string;
    11→  targetType?: "node" | "edge" | "componentDef";
    12→  /** Diff kind for styling */
    13→  diffKind?: DiffKind;
    14→}
    15→
    16→/**
    17→ * Compute a unified diff between two text arrays using a simple LCS-based approach.
    18→ * Returns lines tagged as added/deleted/context.
    19→ */
    20→function computeLineDiff(
    21→  baseLines: string[],
    22→  compareLines: string[]
    23→): { type: "added" | "deleted" | "context"; text: string; baseLine?: number; compareLine?: number }[] {
    24→  const m = baseLines.length;
    25→  const n = compareLines.length;
    26→
    27→  // Build LCS table
    28→  const dp: number[][] = Array.from({ length: m + 1 }, () => Array(n + 1).fill(0));
    29→  for (let i = 1; i <= m; i++) {
    30→    for (let j = 1; j <= n; j++) {
    31→      if (baseLines[i - 1] === compareLines[j - 1]) {
    32→        dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j - 1] + 1;
    33→      } else {
    34→        dp[i][j] = Math.max(dp[i - 1][j], dp[i][j - 1]);
    35→      }
    36→    }
    37→  }
    38→
    39→  // Backtrack to produce diff
    40→  const result: { type: "added" | "deleted" | "context"; text: string; baseLine?: number; compareLine?: number }[] = [];
    41→  let i = m;
    42→  let j = n;
    43→
    44→  const stack: typeof result = [];
    45→  while (i > 0 || j > 0) {
    46→    if (i > 0 && j > 0 && baseLines[i - 1] === compareLines[j - 1]) {
    47→      stack.push({ type: "context", text: baseLines[i - 1], baseLine: i, compareLine: j });
    48→      i--;
    49→      j--;
    50→    } else if (j > 0 && (i === 0 || dp[i][j - 1] >= dp[i - 1][j])) {
    51→      stack.push({ type: "added", text: compareLines[j - 1], compareLine: j });
    52→      j--;
    53→    } else {
    54→      stack.push({ type: "deleted", text: baseLines[i - 1], baseLine: i });
    55→      i--;
    56→    }
    57→  }
    58→
    59→  stack.reverse();
    60→  return stack;
    61→}
    62→
    63→/**
    64→ * Serialize a FlowmaidLayout to YAML text for diff display.
    65→ */
    66→function layoutToYaml(layout: FlowmaidLayout): string {
    67→  return stringify(layout);
    68→}
    69→
    70→/**
    71→ * Try to find which node/edge a YAML line belongs to, for click-to-flash.
    72→ */
    73→function findTargetForLine(
    74→  text: string,
    75→  contextStack: string[]
    76→): { targetId?: string; targetType?: "node" | "edge" | "componentDef" } {
    77→  // Look for node ID in context (e.g. under "nodes:" section, the key is the node ID)
    78→  // contextStack tracks parent keys
    79→  if (contextStack.length >= 2 && contextStack[0] === "nodes") {
    80→    return { targetId: contextStack[1], targetType: "node" };
    81→  }
    82→  if (contextStack.length >= 2 && contextStack[0] === "edges") {
    83→    return { targetId: contextStack[1], targetType: "edge" };
    84→  }
    85→  if (contextStack.length >= 2 && contextStack[0] === "componentDefinitions") {
    86→    return { targetType: "componentDef" };
    87→  }
    88→  return {};
    89→}
    90→
    91→/**
    92→ * Track YAML indentation context to determine which node/edge a line belongs to.
    93→ */
    94→function updateContextStack(line: string, stack: string[]): string[] {
    95→  const trimmed = line.trimStart();
    96→  const indent = line.length - trimmed.length;
    97→
    98→  // Top-level key (indent 0): "nodes:", "edges:", "direction:", "componentDefinitions:"
    99→  if (indent === 0 && trimmed.endsWith(":") && !trimmed.includes(" ")) {
   100→    return [trimmed.slice(0, -1)];
   101→  }
   102→
   103→  // Second-level key (indent 2): node ID or edge ID
   104→  if (indent === 2 && trimmed.endsWith(":") && !trimmed.includes(" ")) {
   105→    return [stack[0] ?? "", trimmed.slice(0, -1)];
   106→  }
   107→
   108→  // Deeper levels: keep current context
   109→  if (stack.length >= 2 && indent > 2) {
   110→    return stack;
   111→  }
   112→
   113→  return stack;
   114→}
   115→
   116→/**
   117→ * Format diff as git-diff-style unified text with line numbers.
   118→ * Shows all lines (unchanged + changed), with +/- markers on changed lines.
   119→ */
   120→export function formatTextDiff(
   121→  result: DiffResult,
   122→  filters: DiffFilters,
   123→  baseLayout?: FlowmaidLayout,
   124→  compareLayout?: FlowmaidLayout
   125→): DiffTextLine[] {
   126→  if (!baseLayout || !compareLayout) return [];
   127→
   128→  const baseYaml = layoutToYaml(baseLayout);
   129→  const compareYaml = layoutToYaml(compareLayout);
   130→
   131→  const baseLines = baseYaml.split("\n");
   132→  const compareLines = compareYaml.split("\n");
   133→
   134→  // Remove trailing empty lines
   135→  while (baseLines.length > 0 && baseLines[baseLines.length - 1] === "") baseLines.pop();
   136→  while (compareLines.length > 0 && compareLines[compareLines.length - 1] === "") compareLines.pop();
   137→
   138→  const rawDiff = computeLineDiff(baseLines, compareLines);
   139→
   140→  const lines: DiffTextLine[] = [];
   141→  let contextStack: string[] = [];
   142→  let lineNum = 0;
   143→
   144→  for (const entry of rawDiff) {
   145→    lineNum++;
   146→    const { targetId, targetType } = findTargetForLine(entry.text, contextStack);
   147→
   148→    // Update context tracking
   149→    if (entry.type === "context" || entry.type === "deleted") {
   150→      contextStack = updateContextStack(entry.text, contextStack);
   151→    }
   152→
   153→    // Apply filters by diff kind
   154→    if (entry.type === "added" && !filters.added) continue;
   155→    if (entry.type === "deleted" && !filters.deleted) continue;
   156→
   157→    const diffLine: DiffTextLine = {
   158→      type: entry.type,
   159→      text: entry.text,
   160→      lineNumber: lineNum,
   161→      targetId,
   162→      targetType,
   163→      diffKind: entry.type === "added" ? "added" : entry.type === "deleted" ? "deleted" : undefined,
   164→    };
   165→
   166→    lines.push(diffLine);
   167→  }
   168→
   169→  return lines;
   170→}
   171→
```

   118→  const [diffFilters, setDiffFilters] = useState<DiffFilters>(DEFAULT_DIFF_FILTERS);
   119→  const [diffResult, setDiffResult] = useState<DiffResult | null>(null);
   120→  const [diffFlashTarget, setDiffFlashTarget] = useState<{ id: string; seq: number } | null>(null);

   130→  useEffect(() => {

   134→
   135→    const handleImportEvent = (e: Event) => {
   136→      try {
   137→        const content = (e as CustomEvent).detail as string;
   138→        const result = deserialize(content);
   139→        useFlowStore.getState().loadState(result);
   140→        toast.success(locales[useLocaleStore.getState().locale]["importedSuccess"]);
   141→      } catch (err) {
   142→        toast.error(locales[useLocaleStore.getState().locale]["importFailed"]);
   143→        console.error(err);
   144→      }
   145→    };
   146→
   147→    window.addEventListener("flowmaid:export", handleExportEvent);
   148→    window.addEventListener("flowmaid:import", handleImportEvent);
   149→    return () => {
   150→      window.removeEventListener("flowmaid:export", handleExportEvent);
   151→      window.removeEventListener("flowmaid:import", handleImportEvent);
   152→    };

   167→      const reader = new FileReader();
   168→      reader.onload = (ev) => {
   169→        const content = ev.target?.result as string;
   170→        window.dispatchEvent(
   171→          new CustomEvent("flowmaid:import", { detail: content })
   172→        );
   173→      };
   174→      reader.readAsText(file);
   175→      e.target.value = "";
   176→    },
   177→    []

   204→    setBulkEditSelectedIds([]);
   205→    setBulkEditFocusTarget({ type: "node", id: nodeId });
   206→    setBulkEditHighlightId(nodeId);
   207→  }, []);
   208→
   209→  const handleBulkEditFocusEdge = useCallback((edgeId: string) => {
   210→    setBulkEditSelectedIds([]);
   211→    setBulkEditFocusTarget({ type: "edge", id: edgeId });
   212→    setBulkEditHighlightId(edgeId);
   213→  }, []);
   214→
   215→  // Canvas click → highlight only (no zoom)
   216→  const handleBulkEditCanvasNodeClick = useCallback((nodeId: string) => {
   217→    setBulkEditSelectedIds([]);
   218→    setBulkEditFocusTarget(null);
   219→    setBulkEditHighlightId(nodeId);
   220→  }, []);
   221→
   222→  const handleBulkEditCanvasEdgeClick = useCallback((edgeId: string) => {
   223→    setBulkEditSelectedIds([]);
   224→    setBulkEditFocusTarget(null);
   225→    setBulkEditHighlightId(edgeId);
   226→  }, []);
   227→
   228→  const handleBulkEditSelectionChange = useCallback((selectedIds: string[]) => {
   229→    setBulkEditSelectedIds(selectedIds);
   230→    if (selectedIds.length > 0) {
   231→      setBulkEditHighlightId(null);
   232→      setBulkEditFocusTarget(null);
   233→    }
   234→  }, []);
   235→
   236→  const handleBulkEditPaneClick = useCallback(() => {
   237→    setBulkEditSelectedIds([]);
   238→    setBulkEditHighlightId(null);
   239→    setBulkEditFocusTarget(null);
   240→  }, []);
   241→
   242→  const diffStepRef = useRef(diffStep);
   243→  diffStepRef.current = diffStep;
   244→
   245→  // --- Diff compare mode handlers ---
   246→  const handleEnterDiffMode = useCallback(() => {
   247→    setIsDiffMode(true);
   248→    setDiffStep("import");
   249→    setDiffBaseLayout(null);
   250→    setDiffBaseFileName(null);
   251→    setDiffBaseMeta(null);
   252→    setDiffCompareLayout(null);
   253→    setDiffCompareFileName(null);
   254→    setDiffCompareMeta(null);
   255→    setDiffResult(null);
   256→    setDiffFilters(DEFAULT_DIFF_FILTERS);
   257→    setDiffFlashTarget(null);
   258→  }, []);
   259→
   260→  const handleExitDiffMode = useCallback(() => {
   261→    if (diffStepRef.current === "compare") {
   262→      setDiffStep("import");
   263→      setDiffResult(null);
   264→      setDiffFlashTarget(null);
   265→    } else {
   266→      setIsDiffMode(false);
   267→    }
   268→  }, []);
   269→
   270→  const handleDiffBaseSelect = useCallback((layout: FlowmaidLayout, fileName: string, meta: FileMeta) => {
   271→    setDiffBaseLayout(layout);
   272→    setDiffBaseFileName(fileName);
   273→    setDiffBaseMeta(meta);
   274→  }, []);
   275→
   276→  const handleDiffCompareSelect = useCallback((layout: FlowmaidLayout, fileName: string, meta: FileMeta) => {
   277→    setDiffCompareLayout(layout);
   278→    setDiffCompareFileName(fileName);
   279→    setDiffCompareMeta(meta);
   280→  }, []);
   281→
   282→  const handleDiffBaseClear = useCallback(() => {
   283→    setDiffBaseLayout(null);
   284→    setDiffBaseFileName(null);
   285→    setDiffBaseMeta(null);
   286→  }, []);
   287→
   288→  const handleDiffCompareClear = useCallback(() => {
   289→    setDiffCompareLayout(null);
   290→    setDiffCompareFileName(null);
   291→    setDiffCompareMeta(null);
   292→  }, []);
   293→
   294→  const handleRunDiff = useCallback(() => {
   295→    if (diffBaseLayout && diffCompareLayout) {
   296→      const result = computeDiff(diffBaseLayout, diffCompareLayout);
   297→      setDiffResult(result);
   298→      setDiffStep("compare");
   299→    }
   300→  }, [diffBaseLayout, diffCompareLayout]);
   301→
   302→  const handleDiffTextItemClick = useCallback((targetId: string, _targetType: "node" | "edge" | "componentDef") => {
   303→    diffFlashSeqRef.current += 1;
   304→    setDiffFlashTarget({ id: targetId, seq: diffFlashSeqRef.current });
   305→  }, []);
   306→
   307→  return (
   308→    <ReactFlowProvider>
   309→      <DnDProvider>
   310→        <div
   311→          className="h-screen w-screen overflow-hidden flex flex-col transition-[padding,background-color] duration-500 ease-in-out"
   312→          style={{
   313→            padding: isEditingComponent ? '0.4% 1% 1% 1%' : '0',
   314→            backgroundColor: isEditingComponent ? 'var(--foreground)' : 'transparent',
   315→          }}
   316→        >
   317→          <div
   318→            className="overflow-hidden transition-[max-height,opacity] duration-500 ease-in-out"
   319→            style={{
   320→              maxHeight: isEditingComponent ? '48px' : '0px',
   321→              opacity: isEditingComponent ? 1 : 0,
   322→            }}
   323→          >
   324→            <ComponentEditingHeader />
   325→          </div>
   326→          <div className={`flex-1 min-h-0 w-full flex flex-col transition-[border-radius] duration-500 ease-in-out overflow-hidden ${isEditingComponent ? 'rounded-lg bg-background' : ''}`}>
   327→          <Toolbar
   328→            onExport={isBulkEditMode || isDiffMode ? undefined : handleExport}
   329→            onImport={isBulkEditMode || isDiffMode ? undefined : handleImport}
   330→            onImportMermaid={isBulkEditMode || isDiffMode ? undefined : handleImportMermaid}
   331→            onFitView={isBulkEditMode || isDiffMode ? undefined : handleFitView}
   332→            isBulkEditMode={isBulkEditMode}
   333→            onEnterBulkEdit={isEditingComponent || isDiffMode ? undefined : handleEnterBulkEdit}
   334→            onExitBulkEdit={handleExitBulkEdit}
   335→            isDiffMode={isDiffMode}
   336→            onEnterDiffMode={isEditingComponent || isBulkEditMode ? undefined : handleEnterDiffMode}
   337→            onExitDiffMode={isDiffMode ? handleExitDiffMode : undefined}
   338→            diffFilterBar={isDiffMode && diffStep === "compare" ? (
   339→              <DiffFilterBar filters={diffFilters} onFiltersChange={setDiffFilters} />
   340→            ) : undefined}
   341→          />
   342→          {!isBulkEditMode && !isDiffMode && <FormatBar />}
   343→          {isDiffMode ? (
   344→            <div className="flex flex-1 overflow-hidden">
   345→              {diffStep === "import" ? (
   346→                <DiffImportPanel
   347→                  baseLayout={diffBaseLayout}
   348→                  baseFileName={diffBaseFileName}
   349→                  baseMeta={diffBaseMeta}
   350→                  compareLayout={diffCompareLayout}
   351→                  compareFileName={diffCompareFileName}
   352→                  compareMeta={diffCompareMeta}
   353→                  onBaseSelect={handleDiffBaseSelect}
   354→                  onCompareSelect={handleDiffCompareSelect}
   355→                  onBaseClear={handleDiffBaseClear}
   356→                  onCompareClear={handleDiffCompareClear}
   357→                  onRunCompare={handleRunDiff}
   358→                  onExit={handleExitDiffMode}
   359→                />
   360→              ) : diffResult && diffBaseLayout && diffCompareLayout ? (
   361→                <ResizablePanelGroup orientation="horizontal" className="flex-1">
   362→                  <ResizablePanel defaultSize={DIFF_CANVAS_DEFAULT_SIZE} minSize={DIFF_CANVAS_MIN_SIZE}>
   363→                    <DiffCanvas
   364→                      baseLayout={diffBaseLayout}
   365→                      compareLayout={diffCompareLayout}
   366→                      diffResult={diffResult}
   367→                      filters={diffFilters}
   368→                      flashTarget={diffFlashTarget}
   369→                      onExit={handleExitDiffMode}
   370→                      baseFileName={diffBaseFileName!}
   371→                      compareFileName={diffCompareFileName!}
   372→                    />
   373→                  </ResizablePanel>
   374→                  <ResizableHandle bare className="w-px bg-border" />
   375→                  <ResizablePanel defaultSize={DIFF_TEXT_DEFAULT_SIZE} minSize={DIFF_TEXT_MIN_SIZE}>
   376→                    <DiffTextPanel
   377→                      diffResult={diffResult}
   378→                      filters={diffFilters}
   379→                      baseLayout={diffBaseLayout!}
   380→                      compareLayout={diffCompareLayout!}
   381→                      onItemClick={handleDiffTextItemClick}
   382→                    />
   383→                  </ResizablePanel>
   384→                </ResizablePanelGroup>
   385→              ) : null}
   386→            </div>
   387→          ) : isBulkEditMode ? (
   388→            <div className="flex flex-1 overflow-hidden">
   389→              <ResizablePanelGroup orientation="horizontal" className="flex-1">
   390→                <ResizablePanel defaultSize={BULK_EDIT_CANVAS_DEFAULT_SIZE} minSize={BULK_EDIT_CANVAS_MIN_SIZE}>
   391→                  <BulkEditCanvas
   392→                    focusTarget={bulkEditFocusTarget}
   393→                    highlightId={bulkEditHighlightId}
   394→                    onNodeClick={handleBulkEditCanvasNodeClick}
   395→                    onEdgeClick={handleBulkEditCanvasEdgeClick}
   396→                    onSelectionChange={handleBulkEditSelectionChange}
   397→                    onPaneClick={handleBulkEditPaneClick}
   398→                    onExit={handleExitBulkEdit}
   399→                  />
   400→                </ResizablePanel>
   401→                <ResizableHandle bare className="w-px bg-border" />
   402→                <ResizablePanel defaultSize={BULK_EDIT_TABLE_DEFAULT_SIZE} minSize={BULK_EDIT_TABLE_MIN_SIZE}>
   403→                  <BulkEditTable
   404→                    onFocusNode={handleBulkEditFocusNode}
   405→                    onFocusEdge={handleBulkEditFocusEdge}
   406→                    highlightId={bulkEditHighlightId}
   407→                    selectedIds={bulkEditSelectedIds}
   408→                  />
   409→                </ResizablePanel>
   410→              </ResizablePanelGroup>
   411→            </div>
   412→          ) : (
   413→          <div className="flex flex-1 overflow-hidden">
   414→            <CollapsiblePanel
   415→              side="left"
   416→              isOpen={leftOpen}
   417→              onToggle={toggleLeft}
   418→              width={leftWidth}
   419→              ribbonLabel="Nodes"
   420→            >
   421→              <NodePalette />
   422→            </CollapsiblePanel>
   423→
   424→            <ResizablePanelGroup orientation="horizontal" className="flex-1">
   425→              <ResizablePanel defaultSize={rightOpen ? 70 : 100} minSize={30}>
   426→                <FlowCanvas />
   427→              </ResizablePanel>
   428→
   429→              {rightOpen && (
   430→                <>
   431→                  <ToggleResizeHandle onClick={toggleRight} />
   432→                  <ResizablePanel defaultSize={30} minSize={15}>
   433→                    <MermaidPreview />
   434→                  </ResizablePanel>
   435→                </>
   436→              )}
   437→            </ResizablePanelGroup>
   438→
   439→            {!rightOpen && (
   440→              <ToggleRibbon onClick={toggleRight} />
   441→            )}
   442→          </div>
   443→          )}
   444→          </div>
   445→        </div>
   446→        <input
   447→          ref={fileInputRef}
   448→          type="file"
   449→          accept=".flowmaid"
   450→          className="hidden"
   451→          onChange={handleFileChange}
   452→        />
   453→        <MermaidImportDialog
   454→          open={mermaidImportOpen}
   455→          onOpenChange={setMermaidImportOpen}
   456→          onSuccess={handleFitView}
   457→        />
   458→        <ExportDialog
   459→          open={exportDialogOpen}
   460→          onOpenChange={setExportDialogOpen}
   461→        />
   462→        <BetaNoticeDialog />
   463→      </DnDProvider>
   464→    </ReactFlowProvider>
   465→  );
   466→}
   467→

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import { useLocale } from "@/lib/i18n/useLocale";
     4→import type { DiffFilters } from "@/lib/diff/types";
     5→
     6→interface DiffFilterBarProps {
     7→  filters: DiffFilters;
     8→  onFiltersChange: (filters: DiffFilters) => void;
     9→}
    10→
    11→export function DiffFilterBar({ filters, onFiltersChange }: DiffFilterBarProps) {
    12→  const { t } = useLocale();
    13→
    14→  const toggle = (key: keyof DiffFilters) => {
    15→    onFiltersChange({ ...filters, [key]: !filters[key] });
    16→  };
    17→
    18→  const items: { key: keyof DiffFilters; label: string }[] = [
    19→    { key: "added", label: t("diffKindAdded") },
    20→    { key: "deleted", label: t("diffKindDeleted") },
    21→    { key: "modified", label: t("diffKindModified") },
    22→  ];
    23→
    24→  return (
    25→    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
    26→      {items.map(({ key, label }) => (
    27→        <label key={key} className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs cursor-pointer select-none">
    28→          <input
    29→            type="checkbox"
    30→            checked={filters[key]}
    31→            onChange={() => toggle(key)}
    32→            className="accent-primary h-3.5 w-3.5"
    33→          />
    34→          {label}
    35→        </label>
    36→      ))}
    37→    </div>
    38→  );
    39→}
    40→

  "file_path": "/root/flowmaid/src/lib/diff/buildDiffNodes.ts",
  "content": "import type { FlowmaidLayout, FlowmaidNodeLayout } from \"@/lib/flowmaid/schema\";\nimport type { DiffKind } from \"./types\";\nimport type { FlowNode, FlowEdge } from \"@/store/types\";\n\n/** Colors for diff highlights */\nexport const DIFF_COLORS: Record<DiffKind, string> = {\n  added: \"#22c55e\",\n  deleted: \"#ef4444\",\n  modified: \"#eab308\",\n  unchanged: \"transparent\",\n};\n\n/** Build a FlowNode from a layout entry, optionally applying extra CSS styles */\nexport function buildNodeFromLayout(\n  id: string,\n  n: FlowmaidNodeLayout,\n  extraStyle?: React.CSSProperties,\n): FlowNode {\n  return {\n    id,\n    type: n.componentDefinitionId ? \"componentInstance\" : n.shape,\n    position: n.position,\n    data: {\n      label: n.label,\n      shape: n.shape as FlowNode[\"data\"][\"shape\"],\n      fillColor: n.fillColor,\n      borderColor: n.borderColor,\n      borderWidth: n.borderWidth,\n      borderStyle: n.borderStyle,\n      fontSize: n.fontSize,\n      textColor: n.textColor,\n      textAlign: n.textAlign,\n      bold: n.bold,\n      italic: n.italic,\n      underline: n.underline,\n    },\n    style: {\n      ...(n.size ? { width: n.size.width, height: n.size.height } : {}),\n      ...extraStyle,\n    },\n  } as FlowNode;\n}\n\n/** Build plain FlowNodes and FlowEdges from a FlowmaidLayout (no diff styling) */\nexport function buildPlainNodesEdges(layout: FlowmaidLayout): {\n  displayNodes: FlowNode[];\n  displayEdges: FlowEdge[];\n} {\n  const nodes: FlowNode[] = [];\n  const edges: FlowEdge[] = [];\n\n  for (const [id, n] of Object.entries(layout.nodes)) {\n    nodes.push(buildNodeFromLayout(id, n));\n  }\n\n  for (const [id, e] of Object.entries(layout.edges)) {\n    edges.push({\n      id,\n      source: e.source,\n      target: e.target,\n      type: \"labeled\",\n      sourceHandle: e.sourceHandle,\n      targetHandle: e.targetHandle,\n      data: {\n        label: e.label ?? \"\",\n        edgeType: e.edgeType ?? \"bezier\",\n        markerEnd: e.markerEnd ?? \"arrowclosed\",\n        markerStart: e.markerStart,\n        strokeWidth: e.strokeWidth,\n        strokeColor: e.strokeColor,\n        strokeStyle: e.strokeStyle,\n      },\n    } as FlowEdge);\n  }\n\n  return { displayNodes: nodes, displayEdges: edges };\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/root/flowmaid/src/components/diffComparison/DiffBadgeOverlay.tsx",
  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useViewport } from \"@xyflow/react\";\nimport type { DiffResult, DiffFilters, DiffKind } from \"@/lib/diff/types\";\nimport type { FlowmaidLayout } from \"@/lib/flowmaid/schema\";\nimport { DIFF_COLORS } from \"@/lib/diff/buildDiffNodes\";\nimport { useLocale } from \"@/lib/i18n/useLocale\";\nimport type { TranslationKey } from \"@/lib/i18n/locales\";\n\n/** Badge size constants */\nconst BADGE_FONT_SIZE = 10;\nconst BADGE_OFFSET_X = 4;\nconst BADGE_OFFSET_Y = -12;\n\ninterface DiffBadgeOverlayProps {\n  diffResult: DiffResult;\n  filters: DiffFilters;\n  layout: FlowmaidLayout;\n  side: \"base\" | \"compare\";\n}\n\nconst KIND_LABEL_KEYS: Record<DiffKind, TranslationKey> = {\n  added: \"diffKindAdded\",\n  deleted: \"diffKindDeleted\",\n  modified: \"diffKindModified\",\n  unchanged: \"diffKindModified\", // unused\n};\n\nexport function DiffBadgeOverlay({ diffResult, filters, layout, side }: DiffBadgeOverlayProps) {\n  const { x, y, zoom } = useViewport();\n  const { t } = useLocale();\n\n  const badges: { nodeId: string; kind: DiffKind; screenX: number; screenY: number }[] = [];\n\n  for (const nd of diffResult.nodeDiffs) {\n    if (nd.kind === \"unchanged\") continue;\n\n    // Filter by kind\n    if (nd.kind === \"added\" && !filters.added) continue;\n    if (nd.kind === \"deleted\" && !filters.deleted) continue;\n    if (nd.kind === \"modified\" && !filters.modified) continue;\n\n    // Base side shows DELETED + MODIFIED, compare side shows ADDED + MODIFIED\n    if (side === \"base\" && nd.kind === \"added\") continue;\n    if (side === \"compare\" && nd.kind === \"deleted\") continue;\n\n    const nodeLayout = layout.nodes[nd.nodeId];\n    if (!nodeLayout) continue;\n\n    const nodeW = nodeLayout.size?.width ?? 150;\n    const screenX = (nodeLayout.position.x + nodeW) * zoom + x + BADGE_OFFSET_X;\n    const screenY = nodeLayout.position.y * zoom + y + BADGE_OFFSET_Y;\n\n    badges.push({ nodeId: nd.nodeId, kind: nd.kind, screenX, screenY });\n  }\n\n  if (badges.length === 0) return null;\n\n  return (\n    <div style={{ position: \"absolute\", top: 0, left: 0, width: \"100%\", height: \"100%\", pointerEvents: \"none\" }}>\n      {badges.map((badge) => {\n        const color = DIFF_COLORS[badge.kind];\n        return (\n          <div\n            key={badge.nodeId}\n            style={{\n              position: \"absolute\",\n              left: badge.screenX,\n              top: badge.screenY,\n              fontSize: BADGE_FONT_SIZE,\n              fontWeight: 700,\n              color: \"#fff\",\n              backgroundColor: color,\n              borderRadius: 4,\n              padding: \"1px 5px\",\n              whiteSpace: \"nowrap\",\n              boxShadow: `0 0 8px ${color}`,\n              lineHeight: 1.4,\n              pointerEvents: \"none\",\n              userSelect: \"none\",\n            }}\n          >\n            {t(KIND_LABEL_KEYS[badge.kind]).toUpperCase()}\n          </div>\n        );\n      })}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

\"@xyflow/react\";\nimport { ArrowLeft } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { useLocale } from \"@/lib/i18n/useLocale\";\nimport { nodeTypes } from \"@/components/nodes/nodeTypes\";\nimport { edgeTypes } from \"@/components/edges/edgeTypes\";\nimport type { FlowmaidLayout } from \"@/lib/flowmaid/schema\";\nimport type { DiffResult, DiffFilters } from \"@/lib/diff/types\";\nimport { buildPlainNodesEdges } from \"@/lib/diff/buildDiffNodes\";\nimport { DiffBadgeOverlay } from \"./DiffBadgeOverlay\";\nimport type { FlowNode, FlowEdge } from \"@/store/types\";\n\n// ─── Types ───\n\ninterface DiffCanvasProps {\n  baseLayout: FlowmaidLayout;\n  compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout;\n  diffResult: DiffResult;\n  filters: DiffFilters;\n  onExit: () => void;\n  baseFileName: string;\n  compareFileName: string;\n}\n\ninterface DiffSingleCanvasProps {\n  layout: FlowmaidLayout;\n  diffResult: DiffResult;\n  filters: DiffFilters;\n  side: \"base\" | \"compare\";\n  fileName: string;\n  /** Shared viewport for sync */\n  sharedViewport: Viewport | null;\n  onViewportChange: (vp: Viewport) => void;\n  /** Ref to prevent sync loops */\n  isSyncingRef: React.MutableRefObject<boolean>;\n  onExit?: () => void;\n}\n\n// ─── DiffSingleCanvas (one side of the side-by-side view) ───\n\nconst DiffSingleCanvas = memo(function DiffSingleCanvas({\n  layout,\n  diffResult,\n  filters,\n  side,\n  fileName,\n  sharedViewport,\n  onViewportChange,\n  isSyncingRef,\n  onExit,\n}: DiffSingleCanvasProps) {\n  const { t } = useLocale();\n  const { setViewport } = useReactFlow();\n  const prevViewportRef = useRef<Viewport | null>(null);\n\n  // Build nodes/edges for this side\n  const { displayNodes, displayEdges } = useMemo(() => {\n    const { displayNodes: allNodes, displayEdges: allEdges } = buildPlainNodesEdges(layout);\n\n    // Filter nodes based on side + diff\n    const filteredNodes: FlowNode[] = [];\n    for (const node of allNodes) {\n      const nd = diffResult.nodeDiffs.find((d) => d.nodeId === node.id);\n      if (!nd || nd.kind === \"unchanged\" || nd.kind === \"modified\") {\n        filteredNodes.push(node);\n        continue;\n      }\n      // base side: hide added nodes, compare side: hide deleted nodes\n      if (side === \"base\" && nd.kind === \"added\") {\n        if (!filters.added) continue;\n        // added node doesn't exist in base — skip\n        continue;\n      }\n      if (side === \"compare\" && nd.kind === \"deleted\") {\n        if (!filters.deleted) continue;\n        // deleted node doesn't exist in compare — skip\n        continue;\n      }\n      // Filter visibility\n      if (nd.kind === \"added\" && !filters.added) continue;\n      if (nd.kind === \"deleted\" && !filters.deleted) continue;\n      filteredNodes.push(node);\n    }\n\n    // Filter edges: only show edges where both endpoints exist in filteredNodes\n    const nodeIdSet = new Set(filteredNodes.map((n) => n.id));\n    const filteredEdges = allEdges.filter(\n      (e) => nodeIdSet.has(e.source) && nodeIdSet.has(e.target),\n    );\n\n    return { displayNodes: filteredNodes, displayEdges: filteredEdges };\n  }, [layout, diffResult, filters, side]);\n\n  // Sync viewport from the other side\n  const handleMoveEnd = useCallback(\n    (_event: unknown, vp: Viewport) => {\n      if (isSyncingRef.current) return;\n      onViewportChange(vp);\n    },\n    [onViewportChange, isSyncingRef],\n  );\n\n  // Apply shared viewport from the other side\n  const lastAppliedRef = useRef<string>(\"\");\n  if (sharedViewport) {\n    const vpKey = `${sharedViewport.x},${sharedViewport.y},${sharedViewport.zoom}`;\n    if (vpKey !== lastAppliedRef.current) {\n      lastAppliedRef.current = vpKey;\n      isSyncingRef.current = true;\n      setViewport(sharedViewport, { duration: 0 });\n      // Reset syncing flag after React Flow processes the viewport change\n      requestAnimationFrame(() => {\n        isSyncingRef.current = false;\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"h-full w-full relative\">\n      <ReactFlow\n        nodes={displayNodes}\n        edges={displayEdges}\n        nodeTypes={nodeTypes}\n        edgeTypes={edgeTypes}\n        nodesDraggable={false}\n        nodesConnectable={false}\n        elementsSelectable={false}\n        connectOnClick={false}\n        zoomOnScroll\n        panOnDrag\n        fitView\n        onMoveEnd={handleMoveEnd}\n        proOptions={{ hideAttribution: true }}\n      >\n        {/* File name label */}\n        <Panel position=\"top-left\">\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n            {onExit && (\n              <button\n                onClick={onExit}\n                className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-background/80 border border-border text-xs text-foreground backdrop-blur-sm hover:bg-accent cursor-pointer\"\n              >\n                <ArrowLeft size={14} />\n                {t(\"diffBack\")}\n              </button>\n            )}\n            <div className=\"px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md bg-background/80 border border-border text-xs text-foreground backdrop-blur-sm\">\n              {fileName}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </Panel>\n\n        {/* Diff badge overlay */}\n        <DiffBadgeOverlay\n          diffResult={diffResult}\n          filters={filters}\n          layout={layout}\n          side={side}\n        />\n      </ReactFlow>\n    </div>\n  );\n});\n\n// ─── DiffCanvas (outer: side-by-side layout with two ReactFlowProviders) ───\n\nexport const DiffCanvas = memo(function DiffCanvas({\n  baseLayout,\n  compareLayout,\n  diffResult,\n  filters,\n  onExit,\n  baseFileName,\n  compareFileName,\n}: DiffCanvasProps) {\n  // Shared viewport state for syncing left ↔ right\n  const baseViewportRef = useRef<Viewport | null>(null);\n  const compareViewportRef = useRef<Viewport | null>(null);\n  const isSyncingRef = useRef(false);\n\n  // When base side changes viewport → update compare side\n  const handleBaseViewportChange = useCallback((vp: Viewport) => {\n    compareViewportRef.current = vp;\n    // Force re-render to propagate\n    setCompareVp({ ...vp });\n  }, []);\n\n  // When compare side changes viewport → update base side\n  const handleCompareViewportChange = useCallback((vp: Viewport) => {\n    baseViewportRef.current = vp;\n    setBaseVp({ ...vp });\n  }, []);\n\n  // State to trigger re-renders for viewport sync\n  const [baseVp, setBaseVp] = React.useState<Viewport | null>(null);\n  const [compareVp, setCompareVp] = React.useState<Viewport | null>(null);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex h-full w-full\">\n      {/* Left: Base canvas */}\n      <div className=\"flex-1 h-full border-r border-border\">\n        <ReactFlowProvider>\n          <DiffSingleCanvas\n            layout={baseLayout}\n            diffResult={diffResult}\n            filters={filters}\n            side=\"base\"\n            fileName={baseFileName}\n            sharedViewport={baseVp}\n            onViewportChange={handleBaseViewportChange}\n            isSyncingRef={isSyncingRef}\n            onExit={onExit}\n          />\n        </ReactFlowProvider>\n      </div>\n      {/* Right: Compare canvas */}\n      <div className=\"flex-1 h-full\">\n        <ReactFlowProvider>\n          <DiffSingleCanvas\n            layout={compareLayout}\n            diffResult={diffResult}\n            filters={filters}\n            side=\"compare\"\n            fileName={compareFileName}\n            sharedViewport={compareVp}\n            onViewportChange={handleCompareViewportChange}\n            isSyncingRef={isSyncingRef}\n          />\n        </ReactFlowProvider>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n});\n"
}
