```
     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
     4→import { useViewport } from "@xyflow/react";
     5→import type { DiffResult, DiffFilters, DiffKind } from "@/lib/diff/types";
     6→import type { FlowmaidLayout } from "@/lib/flowmaid/schema";
     7→import { DIFF_COLORS } from "@/lib/diff/buildDiffNodes";
     8→
     9→/** Duration of flash animation (ms) — matches CSS */
    10→const FLASH_DURATION_MS = 1200;
    11→
    12→/** Glow blur radius for the outer glow */
    13→const GLOW_BLUR = 6;
    14→/** Inner shadow opacity */
    15→const INNER_SHADOW_OPACITY = 0.15;
    16→
    17→interface DiffGlowOverlayProps {
    18→  diffResult: DiffResult;
    19→  filters: DiffFilters;
    20→  /** Flash target for click-to-highlight */
    21→  flashTarget?: { id: string; type: "node" | "edge"; seq: number } | null;
    22→  layout: FlowmaidLayout;
    23→  side: "base" | "compare";
    24→}
    25→
    26→/**
    27→ * SVG shape path generators.
    28→ * All return a path string in the coordinate system (0, 0, width, height).
    29→ */
    30→const SHAPE_PATHS: Record<string, (w: number, h: number) => string> = {
    31→  rectangle: (w, h) => `M 0,0 L ${w},0 L ${w},${h} L 0,${h} Z`,
    32→  text: (w, h) => `M 0,0 L ${w},0 L ${w},${h} L 0,${h} Z`,
    33→  roundedRect: (w, h) => {
    34→    const r = Math.min(16, w / 4, h / 4);
    35→    return `M ${r},0 L ${w - r},0 Q ${w},0 ${w},${r} L ${w},${h - r} Q ${w},${h} ${w - r},${h} L ${r},${h} Q 0,${h} 0,${h - r} L 0,${r} Q 0,0 ${r},0 Z`;
    36→  },
    37→  circle: (w, h) => {
    38→    const rx = w / 2;
    39→    const ry = h / 2;
    40→    return `M ${rx},0 A ${rx},${ry} 0 1,1 ${rx},${h} A ${rx},${ry} 0 1,1 ${rx},0 Z`;
    41→  },
    42→  stadium: (w, h) => {
    43→    const r = h / 2;
    44→    return `M ${r},0 L ${w - r},0 A ${r},${r} 0 0,1 ${w - r},${h} L ${r},${h} A ${r},${r} 0 0,1 ${r},0 Z`;
    45→  },
    46→  diamond: (w, h) =>
    47→    `M ${w / 2},0 L ${w},${h / 2} L ${w / 2},${h} L 0,${h / 2} Z`,
    48→  parallelogram: (w, h) => {
    49→    const offset = w * 0.15;
    50→    return `M ${offset},0 L ${w},0 L ${w - offset},${h} L 0,${h} Z`;
    51→  },
    52→  hexagon: (w, h) => {
    53→    const qw = w * 0.25;
    54→    return `M ${qw},0 L ${w - qw},0 L ${w},${h / 2} L ${w - qw},${h} L ${qw},${h} L 0,${h / 2} Z`;
    55→  },
    56→  trapezoid: (w, h) => {
    57→    const offset = w * 0.2;
    58→    return `M ${offset},0 L ${w - offset},0 L ${w},${h} L 0,${h} Z`;
    59→  },
    60→  cylinder: (w, h) => {
    61→    const ey = h * 0.15;
    62→    return `M 0,${ey} Q 0,0 ${w / 2},0 Q ${w},0 ${w},${ey} L ${w},${h - ey} Q ${w},${h} ${w / 2},${h} Q 0,${h} 0,${h - ey} Z`;
    63→  },
    64→  document: (w, h) => {
    65→    const waveH = h * 0.2;
    66→    const bodyH = h - waveH;
    67→    return `M 0,0 L ${w},0 L ${w},${bodyH} Q ${w * 0.75},${h} ${w / 2},${bodyH} Q ${w * 0.25},${bodyH - waveH} 0,${bodyH} Z`;
    68→  },
    69→  predefinedProcess: (w, h) => `M 0,0 L ${w},0 L ${w},${h} L 0,${h} Z`,
    70→  manualInput: (w, h) => {
    71→    const slant = h * 0.2;
    72→    return `M 0,${slant} L ${w},0 L ${w},${h} L 0,${h} Z`;
    73→  },
    74→  internalStorage: (w, h) => `M 0,0 L ${w},0 L ${w},${h} L 0,${h} Z`,
    75→  display: (w, h) => {
    76→    const cx = w * 0.15;
    77→    return `M ${cx},0 L ${w * 0.8},0 Q ${w},${h / 2} ${w * 0.8},${h} L ${cx},${h} Q 0,${h / 2} ${cx},0 Z`;
    78→  },
    79→};
    80→
    81→/** Default size when node has no explicit size */
    82→const DEFAULT_W = 150;
    83→const DEFAULT_H = 50;
    84→
    85→export function DiffGlowOverlay({ diffResult, filters, flashTarget, layout, side }: DiffGlowOverlayProps) {
    86→  const { x, y, zoom } = useViewport();
    87→
    88→  const items: {
    89→    nodeId: string;
    90→    kind: DiffKind;
    91→    px: number;
    92→    py: number;
    93→    w: number;
    94→    h: number;
    95→    shape: string;
    96→  }[] = [];
    97→
    98→  for (const nd of diffResult.nodeDiffs) {
    99→    if (nd.kind === "unchanged") continue;
   100→    if (nd.kind === "added" && !filters.added) continue;
   101→    if (nd.kind === "deleted" && !filters.deleted) continue;
   102→    if (nd.kind === "modified" && !filters.modified) continue;
   103→
   104→    // Glow only on the relevant side
   105→    if (side === "base" && (nd.kind === "added" || nd.kind === "modified")) continue;
   106→    if (side === "compare" && nd.kind === "deleted") continue;
   107→
   108→    const nodeLayout = layout.nodes[nd.nodeId];
   109→    if (!nodeLayout) continue;
   110→
   111→    const w = nodeLayout.size?.width ?? DEFAULT_W;
   112→    const h = nodeLayout.size?.height ?? DEFAULT_H;
   113→    const px = nodeLayout.position.x;
   114→    const py = nodeLayout.position.y;
   115→    const shape = nodeLayout.shape ?? "rectangle";
   116→
   117→    items.push({ nodeId: nd.nodeId, kind: nd.kind, px, py, w, h, shape });
   118→  }
   119→
   120→  // Flash animation state
   121→  const [flashNodeId, setFlashNodeId] = useState<string | null>(null);
   122→  useEffect(() => {
   123→    if (!flashTarget || flashTarget.type !== "node") return;
   124→    setFlashNodeId(flashTarget.id);
   125→    const timer = setTimeout(() => setFlashNodeId(null), FLASH_DURATION_MS);
   126→    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
   127→  }, [flashTarget]);
   128→
   129→  // Build flash node info (even if not in diff items)
   130→  const flashNode = flashNodeId ? (() => {
   131→    const nodeLayout = layout.nodes[flashNodeId];
   132→    if (!nodeLayout) return null;
   133→    const w = nodeLayout.size?.width ?? DEFAULT_W;
   134→    const h = nodeLayout.size?.height ?? DEFAULT_H;
   135→    const shape = nodeLayout.shape ?? "rectangle";
   136→    // Find diff kind for color
   137→    const nd = diffResult.nodeDiffs.find((d) => d.nodeId === flashNodeId);
   138→    const color = nd && nd.kind !== "unchanged" ? DIFF_COLORS[nd.kind] : "var(--color-primary)";
   139→    return { px: nodeLayout.position.x, py: nodeLayout.position.y, w, h, shape, color };
   140→  })() : null;
   141→
   142→  if (items.length === 0 && !flashNode) return null;
   143→
   144→  return (
   145→    <svg
   146→      style={{
   147→        position: "absolute",
   148→        top: 0,
   149→        left: 0,
   150→        width: "100%",
   151→        height: "100%",
   152→        pointerEvents: "none",
   153→        overflow: "visible",
   154→        zIndex: 9998,
   155→      }}
   156→    >
   157→      <defs>
   158→        {/* Outer glow filter */}
   159→        <filter id="diff-glow-outer" x="-50%" y="-50%" width="200%" height="200%">
   160→          <feGaussianBlur in="SourceGraphic" stdDeviation={GLOW_BLUR} />
   161→        </filter>
   162→        {/* Inner shadow filter */}
   163→        <filter id="diff-inner-shadow" x="-50%" y="-50%" width="200%" height="200%">
   164→          <feComponentTransfer in="SourceAlpha">
   165→            <feFuncA type="table" tableValues="1 0" />
   166→          </feComponentTransfer>
   167→          <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="4" result="shadow" />
   168→          <feFlood floodColor="#000" floodOpacity="0.4" result="color" />
   169→          <feComposite in="color" in2="shadow" operator="in" result="innerShadow" />
   170→          <feComposite in="innerShadow" in2="SourceAlpha" operator="in" />
   171→        </filter>
   172→      </defs>
   173→
   174→      <g transform={`translate(${x},${y}) scale(${zoom})`}>
   175→        {items.map((item) => {
   176→          const pathFn = SHAPE_PATHS[item.shape] ?? SHAPE_PATHS.rectangle;
   177→          const d = pathFn(item.w, item.h);
   178→          const color = DIFF_COLORS[item.kind];
   179→
   180→          // Inset margin to keep inner shadow inside the border
   181→          const inset = 3;
   182→          const sx = (item.w - inset * 2) / item.w;
   183→          const sy = (item.h - inset * 2) / item.h;
   184→
   185→          return (
   186→            <g key={item.nodeId} transform={`translate(${item.px},${item.py})`}>
   187→              {/* Solid border in diff color */}
   188→              <path d={d} fill="none" stroke={color} strokeWidth={3} />
   189→              {/* Outer glow (stroke only, no fill) */}
   190→              <path d={d} fill="none" stroke={color} strokeWidth={4} opacity={0.6} filter="url(#diff-glow-outer)" />
   191→              {/* Inner shadow (slightly inset to avoid covering the border) */}
   192→              <g transform={`translate(${inset},${inset}) scale(${sx},${sy})`}>
   193→                <path d={d} fill="black" filter="url(#diff-inner-shadow)" />
   194→              </g>
   195→            </g>
   196→          );
   197→        })}
   198→
   199→        {/* Flash ring wave animation */}
   200→        {flashNode && (
   201→          <g transform={`translate(${flashNode.px},${flashNode.py})`}>
   202→            <path
   203→              d={(SHAPE_PATHS[flashNode.shape] ?? SHAPE_PATHS.rectangle)(flashNode.w, flashNode.h)}
   204→              fill="none"
   205→              stroke={flashNode.color}
   206→              strokeWidth={2}
   207→              className="diff-svg-ring-wave"
   208→            />
   209→          </g>
   210→        )}
   211→      </g>
   212→    </svg>
   213→  );
   214→}
   215→
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/root/flowmaid/src/components/diffComparison/DiffGlowOverlay.tsx",
  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useMemo, useState, useEffect } from \"react\";\nimport { useViewport } from \"@xyflow/react\";\nimport type { DiffResult, DiffFilters, DiffKind } from \"@/lib/diff/types\";\nimport { shouldHighlightOnSide, DIFF_FLASH_DURATION_MS } from \"@/lib/diff/types\";\nimport type { FlowmaidLayout } from \"@/lib/flowmaid/schema\";\nimport { DIFF_COLORS } from \"@/lib/diff/buildDiffNodes\";\n\n/** Glow blur radius for the outer glow */\nconst GLOW_BLUR = 6;\n\n/** Inset margin to keep inner shadow inside the border (px) */\nconst INNER_SHADOW_INSET = 3;\n\ninterface DiffGlowOverlayProps {\n  diffResult: DiffResult;\n  filters: DiffFilters;\n  /** Flash target for click-to-highlight */\n  flashTarget?: { id: string; type: \"node\" | \"edge\"; seq: number } | null;\n  layout: FlowmaidLayout;\n  side: \"base\" | \"compare\";\n}\n\n/**\n * SVG shape path generators.\n * All return a path string in the coordinate system (0, 0, width, height).\n */\nconst rectPath = (w: number, h: number) => `M 0,0 L ${w},0 L ${w},${h} L 0,${h} Z`;\n\nconst SHAPE_PATHS: Record<string, (w: number, h: number) => string> = {\n  rectangle: rectPath,\n  text: rectPath,\n  predefinedProcess: rectPath,\n  internalStorage: rectPath,\n  roundedRect: (w, h) => {\n    const r = Math.min(16, w / 4, h / 4);\n    return `M ${r},0 L ${w - r},0 Q ${w},0 ${w},${r} L ${w},${h - r} Q ${w},${h} ${w - r},${h} L ${r},${h} Q 0,${h} 0,${h - r} L 0,${r} Q 0,0 ${r},0 Z`;\n  },\n  circle: (w, h) => {\n    const rx = w / 2;\n    const ry = h / 2;\n    return `M ${rx},0 A ${rx},${ry} 0 1,1 ${rx},${h} A ${rx},${ry} 0 1,1 ${rx},0 Z`;\n  },\n  stadium: (w, h) => {\n    const r = h / 2;\n    return `M ${r},0 L ${w - r},0 A ${r},${r} 0 0,1 ${w - r},${h} L ${r},${h} A ${r},${r} 0 0,1 ${r},0 Z`;\n  },\n  diamond: (w, h) =>\n    `M ${w / 2},0 L ${w},${h / 2} L ${w / 2},${h} L 0,${h / 2} Z`,\n  parallelogram: (w, h) => {\n    const offset = w * 0.15;\n    return `M ${offset},0 L ${w},0 L ${w - offset},${h} L 0,${h} Z`;\n  },\n  hexagon: (w, h) => {\n    const qw = w * 0.25;\n    return `M ${qw},0 L ${w - qw},0 L ${w},${h / 2} L ${w - qw},${h} L ${qw},${h} L 0,${h / 2} Z`;\n  },\n  trapezoid: (w, h) => {\n    const offset = w * 0.2;\n    return `M ${offset},0 L ${w - offset},0 L ${w},${h} L 0,${h} Z`;\n  },\n  cylinder: (w, h) => {\n    const ey = h * 0.15;\n    return `M 0,${ey} Q 0,0 ${w / 2},0 Q ${w},0 ${w},${ey} L ${w},${h - ey} Q ${w},${h} ${w / 2},${h} Q 0,${h} 0,${h - ey} Z`;\n  },\n  document: (w, h) => {\n    const waveH = h * 0.2;\n    const bodyH = h - waveH;\n    return `M 0,0 L ${w},0 L ${w},${bodyH} Q ${w * 0.75},${h} ${w / 2},${bodyH} Q ${w * 0.25},${bodyH - waveH} 0,${bodyH} Z`;\n  },\n  manualInput: (w, h) => {\n    const slant = h * 0.2;\n    return `M 0,${slant} L ${w},0 L ${w},${h} L 0,${h} Z`;\n  },\n  display: (w, h) => {\n    const cx = w * 0.15;\n    return `M ${cx},0 L ${w * 0.8},0 Q ${w},${h / 2} ${w * 0.8},${h} L ${cx},${h} Q 0,${h / 2} ${cx},0 Z`;\n  },\n};\n\n/** Default size when node has no explicit size */\nconst DEFAULT_W = 150;\nconst DEFAULT_H = 50;\n\ninterface GlowItem {\n  nodeId: string;\n  kind: DiffKind;\n  px: number;\n  py: number;\n  w: number;\n  h: number;\n  shape: string;\n}\n\nexport function DiffGlowOverlay({ diffResult, filters, flashTarget, layout, side }: DiffGlowOverlayProps) {\n  const { x, y, zoom } = useViewport();\n\n  // Memoize glow items — only recompute when diff/filters/layout/side change (not on viewport)\n  const items = useMemo(() => {\n    const result: GlowItem[] = [];\n    for (const nd of diffResult.nodeDiffs) {\n      if (!shouldHighlightOnSide(nd.kind, filters, side)) continue;\n      const nodeLayout = layout.nodes[nd.nodeId];\n      if (!nodeLayout) continue;\n      result.push({\n        nodeId: nd.nodeId,\n        kind: nd.kind,\n        px: nodeLayout.position.x,\n        py: nodeLayout.position.y,\n        w: nodeLayout.size?.width ?? DEFAULT_W,\n        h: nodeLayout.size?.height ?? DEFAULT_H,\n        shape: nodeLayout.shape ?? \"rectangle\",\n      });\n    }\n    return result;\n  }, [diffResult, filters, layout, side]);\n\n  // Flash animation state\n  const [flashNodeId, setFlashNodeId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!flashTarget || flashTarget.type !== \"node\") return;\n    setFlashNodeId(flashTarget.id);\n    const timer = setTimeout(() => setFlashNodeId(null), DIFF_FLASH_DURATION_MS);\n    return () => clearTimeout(timer);\n  }, [flashTarget]);\n\n  // Flash node info (memoized, avoids IIFE in render)\n  const flashNode = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!flashNodeId) return null;\n    const nodeLayout = layout.nodes[flashNodeId];\n    if (!nodeLayout) return null;\n    const nd = diffResult.nodeDiffs.find((d) => d.nodeId === flashNodeId);\n    const color = nd && nd.kind !== \"unchanged\" ? DIFF_COLORS[nd.kind] : \"var(--color-primary)\";\n    return {\n      px: nodeLayout.position.x,\n      py: nodeLayout.position.y,\n      w: nodeLayout.size?.width ?? DEFAULT_W,\n      h: nodeLayout.size?.height ?? DEFAULT_H,\n      shape: nodeLayout.shape ?? \"rectangle\",\n      color,\n    };\n  }, [flashNodeId, layout, diffResult]);\n\n  if (items.length === 0 && !flashNode) return null;\n\n  return (\n    <svg\n      style={{\n        position: \"absolute\",\n        top: 0,\n        left: 0,\n        width: \"100%\",\n        height: \"100%\",\n        pointerEvents: \"none\",\n        overflow: \"visible\",\n        zIndex: 9998,\n      }}\n    >\n      <defs>\n        <filter id=\"diff-glow-outer\" x=\"-50%\" y=\"-50%\" width=\"200%\" height=\"200%\">\n          <feGaussianBlur in=\"SourceGraphic\" stdDeviation={GLOW_BLUR} />\n        </filter>\n        <filter id=\"diff-inner-shadow\" x=\"-50%\" y=\"-50%\" width=\"200%\" height=\"200%\">\n          <feComponentTransfer in=\"SourceAlpha\">\n            <feFuncA type=\"table\" tableValues=\"1 0\" />\n          </feComponentTransfer>\n          <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation=\"4\" result=\"shadow\" />\n          <feFlood floodColor=\"#000\" floodOpacity=\"0.4\" result=\"color\" />\n          <feComposite in=\"color\" in2=\"shadow\" operator=\"in\" result=\"innerShadow\" />\n          <feComposite in=\"innerShadow\" in2=\"SourceAlpha\" operator=\"in\" />\n        </filter>\n      </defs>\n\n      <g transform={`translate(${x},${y}) scale(${zoom})`}>\n        {items.map((item) => {\n          const pathFn = SHAPE_PATHS[item.shape] ?? SHAPE_PATHS.rectangle;\n          const d = pathFn(item.w, item.h);\n          const color = DIFF_COLORS[item.kind];\n          const sx = (item.w - INNER_SHADOW_INSET * 2) / item.w;\n          const sy = (item.h - INNER_SHADOW_INSET * 2) / item.h;\n\n          return (\n            <g key={item.nodeId} transform={`translate(${item.px},${item.py})`}>\n              <path d={d} fill=\"none\" stroke={color} strokeWidth={3} />\n              <path d={d} fill=\"none\" stroke={color} strokeWidth={4} opacity={0.6} filter=\"url(#diff-glow-outer)\" />\n              <g transform={`translate(${INNER_SHADOW_INSET},${INNER_SHADOW_INSET}) scale(${sx},${sy})`}>\n                <path d={d} fill=\"black\" filter=\"url(#diff-inner-shadow)\" />\n              </g>\n            </g>\n          );\n        })}\n\n        {/* Flash ring wave animation */}\n        {flashNode && (\n          <g transform={`translate(${flashNode.px},${flashNode.py})`}>\n            <path\n              d={(SHAPE_PATHS[flashNode.shape] ?? SHAPE_PATHS.rectangle)(flashNode.w, flashNode.h)}\n              fill=\"none\"\n              stroke={flashNode.color}\n              strokeWidth={2}\n              className=\"diff-svg-ring-wave\"\n            />\n          </g>\n        )}\n      </g>\n    </svg>\n  );\n}\n"
}
```

```
    57→    badges.push({ nodeId: nd.nodeId, kind: nd.kind, screenX, screenY });
```

```json
  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useMemo } from \"react\";\nimport { useViewport } from \"@xyflow/react\";\nimport type { DiffResult, DiffFilters, DiffKind } from \"@/lib/diff/types\";\nimport { shouldHighlightOnSide } from \"@/lib/diff/types\";\nimport type { FlowmaidLayout } from \"@/lib/flowmaid/schema\";\nimport { DIFF_COLORS } from \"@/lib/diff/buildDiffNodes\";\nimport { useLocale } from \"@/lib/i18n/useLocale\";\nimport type { TranslationKey } from \"@/lib/i18n/locales\";\n\n/** Badge size constants */\nconst BADGE_FONT_SIZE = 11;\n/** Horizontal offset from node right edge (px, screen space) */\nconst BADGE_OFFSET_X = -32;\n/** Vertical offset from node top edge (px, screen space — negative = above) */\nconst BADGE_OFFSET_Y = -14;\n\ninterface DiffBadgeOverlayProps {\n  diffResult: DiffResult;\n  filters: DiffFilters;\n  layout: FlowmaidLayout;\n  side: \"base\" | \"compare\";\n}\n\nconst KIND_LABEL_KEYS: Record<Exclude<DiffKind, \"unchanged\">, TranslationKey> = {\n  added: \"diffKindAdded\",\n  deleted: \"diffKindDeleted\",\n  modified: \"diffKindModified\",\n};\n\nexport function DiffBadgeOverlay({ diffResult, filters, layout, side }: DiffBadgeOverlayProps) {\n  const { x, y, zoom } = useViewport();\n  const { t } = useLocale();\n\n  // Memoize badge data — only recompute when diff/filters/layout/side change\n  const badgeItems = useMemo(() => {\n    const result: { nodeId: string; kind: Exclude<DiffKind, \"unchanged\">; nodeW: number; px: number; py: number }[] = [];\n    for (const nd of diffResult.nodeDiffs) {\n      if (!shouldHighlightOnSide(nd.kind, filters, side)) continue;\n      const nodeLayout = layout.nodes[nd.nodeId];\n      if (!nodeLayout) continue;\n      result.push({\n        nodeId: nd.nodeId,\n        kind: nd.kind as Exclude<DiffKind, \"unchanged\">,\n        nodeW: nodeLayout.size?.width ?? 150,\n        px: nodeLayout.position.x,\n        py: nodeLayout.position.y,\n      });\n    }\n    return result;\n  }, [diffResult, filters, layout, side]);\n\n  if (badgeItems.length === 0) return null;\n\n  return (\n    <div style={{ position: \"absolute\", top: 0, left: 0, width: \"100%\", height: \"100%\", pointerEvents: \"none\", zIndex: 9999 }}>\n      {badgeItems.map((badge) => {\n        const color = DIFF_COLORS[badge.kind];\n        const screenX = (badge.px + badge.nodeW) * zoom + x + BADGE_OFFSET_X;\n        const screenY = badge.py * zoom + y + BADGE_OFFSET_Y;\n        return (\n          <div\n            key={badge.nodeId}\n            style={{\n              position: \"absolute\",\n              left: screenX,\n              top: screenY,\n              fontSize: BADGE_FONT_SIZE,\n              fontWeight: 700,\n              color: \"#fff\",\n              backgroundColor: color,\n              borderRadius: 4,\n              padding: \"1px 5px\",\n              whiteSpace: \"nowrap\",\n              boxShadow: `0 0 8px ${color}`,\n              lineHeight: 1.4,\n              pointerEvents: \"none\",\n              userSelect: \"none\",\n            }}\n          >\n            {t(KIND_LABEL_KEYS[badge.kind]).toUpperCase()}\n          </div>\n        );\n      })}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}
```

-### Phase 4〜5（未実装）
+### Phase 4 — 実装済み
+
+- [x] 差分比較機能（サイドバイサイド方式）
+  - 2ステップ画面構成: Step 1（ファイル選択） → Step 2（差分比較表示）
+  - Step 1: 左右ファイル選択エリア（ドラッグ&ドロップ、「現在の編集内容を使用」ボタン（左側のみ）、クリアボタン）
+  - Step 2: 上部にサイドバイサイドキャンバス（ベース/比較先）、下部にMermaidソースコード差分
+  - サイドバイサイドキャンバス: 左=ベース、右=比較先、ビューポート同期（ズーム/パン連動）
+  - 差分検出: ノードIDベースで全フィールド比較（ラベル、形状、色、枠線、フォント、位置、サイズ等）、エッジはsource+target+handleの組み合わせ、コンポーネントは定義IDベース
+  - 差分強調表示:
+    - DiffGlowOverlay: SVGオーバーレイで形状に沿った外側グロー（`feGaussianBlur`）+ 内側影（`feComposite`）+ 枠線。15種類の形状すべてに対応
+    - DiffBadgeOverlay: ノード右上にADDED/DELETED/MODIFIEDバッジ（差分カラー+グロー）
+    - 差分カラー: 追加=#22c55e（緑）、削除=#ef4444（赤）、変更=#f59e0b（オレンジ）
+  - 表示ルール: 削除→ベース側のみ強調、追加/変更→比較先側のみ強調
+  - フィルタ: 追加/削除/変更の3種チェックボックス（強調表示の有無切替、ノード自体は常に表示）
+  - フロー透過度スライダー: 元のフロー全体の透過度を調整（0〜100%）、強調表示は影響なし
+  - Mermaidソースコード差分: 統合(Unified)/分割(Split)切替、LCSアルゴリズムによる行差分
+  - テキスト差分クリック → キャンバス上の該当ノード/エッジが3回点滅（ノード行→ノード点滅、エッジ行→エッジ点滅）
+  - ソースコード差分パネル: 折りたたみ可能（ヘッダーは常時表示）、展開ボタンでトグル
+  - 仕様書: [specs/diff-comparison.md](specs/diff-comparison.md)
+

```json
}
```
